**§16 Conversation (10 turns; homework).**
1. छात्रः — गुरो, नयतीत्यत्र को विकरणः? (*guro, nayatīty atra ko vikaraṇaḥ?*) — pause: गुरो, नयति इति
   अत्र कः विकरणः? — "Teacher, in nayati, what is the class sign?"
2. गुरुः — शबिति विकरणः। (*śab iti vikaraṇaḥ.*) — pause: शप् इति विकरणः। — "The class sign is śap."
3. छात्रः — शकारः पकारश्च कुत्र? (*śakāraḥ pakāraś ca kutra?*) — pause: शकारः पकारः च कुत्र? — "Where
   are the ś and the p?"
4. गुरुः — तावितौ, अकार एव तिष्ठति। (*tāv itau, akāra eva tiṣṭhati.*) — pause: तौ इतौ, अकारः एव
   तिष्ठति। — "Those two are markers; only the a stays."
5. छात्रः — नीत्यत्र ईकारः कथमेकारः? (*nīty atra īkāraḥ katham ekāraḥ?*) — pause: नी इति अत्र ईकारः
   कथम् एकारः? — "In nī, how does the ī become e?"
6. गुरुः — गुणेन। (*guṇena.*) — pause: the same — "By guṇa."
7. छात्रः — गच्छतीत्यत्र को धातुः? (*gacchatīty atra ko dhātuḥ?*) — pause: गच्छति इति अत्र कः धातुः? —
   "In gacchati, what is the root?"
8. गुरुः — गमिति धातुः, गच्छेत्यादेशः। (*gam iti dhātuḥ, gacchety ādeśaḥ.*) — pause: गम् इति धातुः, गच्छ
   इति आदेशः। — "The root is gam; gaccha is a substitute."
9. छात्रः — तिष्ठतीत्यत्रापि? (*tiṣṭhatīty atrāpi?*) — pause: तिष्ठति इति अत्र अपि? — "In tiṣṭhati
   too?"
10. गुरुः — आम्, स्थेति धातुः, तिष्ठेत्यादेशः। (*ām, stheti dhātuḥ, tiṣṭhety ādeśaḥ.*) — pause: आम्, स्था
    इति धातुः, तिष्ठ इति आदेशः। — "Yes: the root is sthā, tiṣṭha a substitute."
Words owned: गुरुः, कः, कुत्र, कथम्, एव, अपि, आम्, तौ, आदेशः (c01.l03), गुणः (गुणेन: instr. ✓), तिष्ठति;
labels: विकरणः, शकारः, पकारः, अकारः, ईकारः, एकारः, इत् (इतौ: dual of the label, ○), शप्; today's
धातुः (l01). Seams: नयतीत्यत्र, को विकरणः, शबिति, पकारश्च, तावितौ (औ + इ → आव्), अकार एव, नीत्यत्र (ई
+ इ … नी + इति → नीति: ई + इ → ई, savarṇa), कथमेकारः, गमिति, गच्छेत्यादेशः, अत्रापि, स्थेति,
तिष्ठेत्यादेशः. (Line 5's नीत्यत्र: नी + इति → नीति (6.1.101), + अत्र → नीत्यत्र; Pass A checks.) None of
the exam's items.

**§17 Story — the serial, c03 episode 2: the crocodile's answer (our retelling; the original line is
Panc 4, `vispancu.htm` 5230, *mā maivaṃ vada*).**
1. मकरो वदति — "मा मैवं वद। वानरो मम सुहृत्।" (*makaro vadati — "mā maivaṃ vada. vānaro mama
   suhṛt."*) — pause: मकरः वदति — "मा मा एवम् वद। वानरः मम सुहृत्।" — "The crocodile says: 'No, no,
   do not speak so. The monkey is my friend.'"
2. "स मह्यं सदा मधुराणि फलानि प्रयच्छति।" (*"sa mahyaṃ sadā madhurāṇi phalāni prayacchati."*) — pause:
   "सः मह्यम् सदा मधुराणि फलानि प्रयच्छति।" — "'He always gives me sweet fruits.'"
3. पत्नी न शृणोति। सा क्रुद्धा तिष्ठति। (*patnī na śṛṇoti. sā kruddhā tiṣṭhati.*) — pause: the same —
   "The wife does not listen. She stands there angry."
4. मकरस्य मनः सीदति। (*makarasya manaḥ sīdati.*) — pause: the same — "The crocodile's heart sinks."
5. स तीरं तरति, वानरं च चिन्तयति। (*sa tīraṃ tarati, vānaraṃ ca cintayati.*) — pause: सः तीरम् तरति,
   वानरम् च चिन्तयति। — "He crosses to the bank, and thinks of the monkey."
Gloss: मा मैवं वद "no, no, do not speak so" (मा + मा + एवम्; मा + imperative owned, v3.c03.l04). Words
owned: as ep. 1 + शृणोति, क्रुद्धः (क्रुद्धा: f. ✓), तिष्ठति, मनः, तीरम्, न; today's सीदति, तरति. Seams: मकरो
वदति, मैवं (आ + ए → ऐ), वानरो मम, स मह्यं, मधुराणि (the ण by 8.4.1–2: र … आ … ण, c02), मकरस्य मनः, स
तीरं, वानरं च. (Note: तीरं तरति "crosses to the bank" — Apte s.v. tṝ "To cross over"; Pass C: accept, or
change to समुद्रं तरति.)

**§18 Exercises.**
- **Ex 1 (obj 1; class 6 min)** — "For each verb write the class (by its first root), the class sign
  and the present stem": (1) भवति (2) याति (3) ददाति (4) नृत्यति (5) आप्नोति (6) क्षिपति (7) करोति (8)
  गृह्णाति (9) कथयति (10) स्वपिति (11) जायते (12) लिखति. Key (table 0.C): (1) 1 · शप् · भव (2) 2 · none ·
  या (3) 3 · none (श्लु; doubled) · ददा-/दद्- (4) 4 · श्यन् · नृत्य (5) 5 · श्नु · आप्नु-/आप्नो- (6) 6 · श ·
  क्षिप (7) 8 · उ · कुरु-/करो- (8) 9 · श्ना · गृह्णा-/गृह्णी- (9) 10 · णिच् + शप् · कथय (10) 2 · none · स्वप्
  (+ इट्, 7.2.76) (11) 4 · श्यन् · जाय (7.3.79) (12) 6 · श · लिख. Three points per verb; either grade
  accepted for 3, 5, 7, 8. (Seven of the twelve are also verbs of S0-ec, the +1-day item this lesson books for l03: याति, ददाति, नृत्यति, आप्नोति, करोति, गृह्णाति, कथयति. The repeat is retrieval by design.)
- **Ex 2 (obj 2; class 4 min)** — "Give the stem and its rule": (1) गच्छति (2) इच्छति (3) पिबति (4)
  तिष्ठति (5) पश्यति (6) सीदति (7) जानाति. Key: the card (7.3.77 ×2; 7.3.78 ×4; 7.3.79). One point each.
- **Ex 3 (obj 3; derive it yourself; homework 12 min)** — "Derive, according to Pāṇini, in table form":
  (1) स्मृ → स्मरति (2) जि → जयति (3) हृ → हरति (4) तॄ → तरति (5) चर् → चरति (6) वद् → वदति. Key: E-e
  (1)–(6). One point per correct step line; the trap items (5)–(6) score the "no guṇa" line.
- **Ex 4 (obj 4; production; homework 8 min)** — "In Sanskrit, joined, with the pause form": (1) "The
  boy crosses the river." (2) "Truth prevails." (3) "The deer graze in the forest." (4) "The mind sinks
  with sorrow." (5) "At sunrise the seers get up." (6) "The hunter takes away the deer." Key: (1)
  बालको नदीं तरति। — pause: बालकः नदीम् तरति। (2) सत्यं जयति। (3) मृगा वने चरन्ति। — pause: मृगाः वने
  चरन्ति। (4) दुःखेन मनः सीदति। (5) सूर्योदये ऋषय उत्तिष्ठन्ति। — pause: सूर्य-उदये ऋषयः उत्तिष्ठन्ति।
  (6) व्याधो मृगं हरति। — pause: व्याधः मृगम् हरति। Accept any correct order; accept a plural "deer"
  (मृगान्) in (6).
- **Ex 5 (the sort; class 4 min)** — "Sort into thematic (-अ- stem) and athematic: भवति, अस्ति, नृत्यति,
  ददाति, पृच्छति, शृणोति, करोति, कथयति, गृह्णाति, स्वपिति." Key: thematic — भवति, नृत्यति, पृच्छति,
  कथयति; athematic — अस्ति, ददाति, शृणोति, करोति, गृह्णाति, स्वपिति. (Serves obj 1; 10 items.)
- **Ex 6 (§9–§11; homework 2 min, recorded)** — "Read the नयति table aloud, naming each marker without
  pronouncing it in the result." Key: row 0.36.

**§19 Quiz (10; homework).**
1. According to Pāṇini (3.4.113), ति is sārvadhātuka because: (a) it is a tiṅ (b) it has the marker श्
   (c) it follows a vowel (d) it is P — a. TRADITION
2. According to Pāṇini (3.1.68), शप् comes: (a) between the root and a sārvadhātuka ending (b) before
   the root (c) after the ending (d) only in the past — a. TRADITION
3. In शप्, what is heard is: (a) श (b) अ (c) प (d) शप — b. TRADITION (1.3.8, 1.3.3)
4. According to Pāṇini, नी becomes ने before the class sign by: (a) guṇa, 7.3.84 (b) vṛddhi (c) an
   augment (d) 6.1.78 — a. TRADITION
5. ने + अ + ति → नयति by: (a) 6.1.78 एचोऽयवायावः (b) 7.3.84 (c) 1.1.51 (d) 3.1.68 — a. TRADITION
6. The stem तिष्ठ- of तिष्ठति is, according to Pāṇini: (a) a substitute for स्था (7.3.78) (b) an
   irregular form (c) the root itself (d) a class-3 doubling — a. TRADITION
7. Which verb has no guṇa before its class sign? (a) चरति (b) नयति (c) जयति (d) स्मरति — a. TRADITION
8. Which class sign makes a -य- stem? (a) श्यन् (b) शप् (c) श्ना (d) उ — a. TRADITION
9. According to Pāṇini, the tenth class adds: (a) णिच् (with शप्) (b) श (c) श्नम् (d) nothing — a.
   TRADITION
10. Whitney treats the tenth class as: (a) a causative-type formation, "not limited to the
    present-system" (b) an athematic class (c) the commonest class (d) a Vedic class — a. FACT
    [REF:whitney1889 §607]

**§20 bookings (verbatim).**
- +1 day: ten verbs classified — for गच्छति, नृत्यति, पृच्छति, आप्नोति, करोति, गृह्णाति, कथयति, याति,
  ददाति and मन्यते name the class (by its first root), the class sign and the present stem;
  v4.c03.l03's Station 0 runs exactly this.
- +1 week: नयति unaided — derive, according to Pāṇini, in table form, नी → नयति, every step with its
  sūtra, from memory; v4.c03.l04's Station 0 runs exactly this.

**Class timing (45).** 0–7 Station 0 (S0-ea 3, S0-dh 4) · 7–10 review prompts (four) · 10–12 hook (नयति,
तिष्ठति, गच्छति) · 12–17 3.4.113, 3.1.68, 1.3.8, 7.3.84 with padaccheda (4); the TRADITION/FACT line (1) ·
17–23 E-d with the teacher (5); E-f (1) · 23–27 the sort, Ex 5 (4) · 27–30 the class card shown (3) ·
30–36 Ex 1 (6) · 36–39 the substitute-stem card (3) · 39–43 Ex 2 (4) · 43–45 model sentences read aloud
(2). Homework: Ex 3, Ex 4, Ex 6, §15, §16, §17, the quiz.

**§21 Homework (45).** Ex 3 (12) · Ex 4 written and said (8) · Ex 6 (2) · §15 cards read (3) · §16 aloud
(4) · §17 read (3) · model sentences 1–6 joined then pause form, recorded (3) · the quiz (7) and key (2)

card for your wall. = 45.

**Teacher Notes.** K15–K24, L4, L5 with their corrections; 6.1.65 (णीञ् → नी); 6.1.73 + 6.1.71 (the त्
of गच्छ, इच्छ, यच्छ); 1.3.19 विपराभ्यां जेः (जयते); the दाण् → यच्छ line; 1.1.52's Kāśikā locus (Pass C);

## 3. L3 — पित् ङित्: Strong and Weak, What the p Marker Does (grammar)

**Front matter.** id `v4.c03.l03` · file `lesson-03-strong-and-weak.md` · title sa पित् ङित् · sa_iast
*pit ṅit* · en "Strong and Weak: What the p Marker Does" · hi "सबल और निर्बल: प् इत् क्या करता है" ·
lesson_type grammar · cefr B1 · duration 45 · self_study_minutes **45** · prerequisites [v4.c03.l02] ·
review_of [v4.c03.l02, v4.c03.l01, v3.c03.l01, v3.c03.l03, v3.c03.l04, v2.c04.l01] · vocabulary_count
**4** · tags [grammar, panini, anubandha, pit, nit, strong-weak, athematic, r4-closed, ir-022,
derivation, spaced-review] · media (planned): image `v4c03l03-strong-and-weak.svg` (one stem, two
doors: तिप् सिप् मिप् wearing a प् badge open onto शृणो-; every other ending onto शृणु-/शृण्व्-); audio
`v4c03l03-derivation-read.mp3`, `v4c03l03-conversation.mp3`; worksheet `v4c03l03-strong-weak-cells.pdf`;
quizzes `v4c03l03-quiz.md`, `v4c03l03-quiz-key.md`.

(review_of: the plan's "v3.c03.l01–l04 (R4 cells)" is spelled out as the three lessons whose R4 cells
§3 reaches; §8, deviation 10.)

**Objectives.**
1. en "Say, according to Pāṇini, for twelve affixes whether each is pit, and so whether the stem
   before it is strong or weak." · hi "पाणिनि के अनुसार बारह प्रत्ययों के लिए बताएँ कि कौन पित् है, और
   इसलिए उससे पहले अङ्ग सबल है या निर्बल।" (Ex 1, 12 items)
2. en "Fill twelve present, imperfect and imperative cells of the owned athematic verbs." · hi "परिचित
   अदन्त-भिन्न क्रियाओं के बारह लट्, लङ् और लोट् रूप भरें।" (Ex 2, 12 items)
3. en "Derive, according to Pāṇini, शृणोति and शृण्वन्ति with the teacher, and करोति, कुरुतः, कुर्वन्ति,
   अस्ति, सन्ति and पृच्छति by yourself." · hi "पाणिनि के अनुसार शिक्षक के साथ शृणोति और शृण्वन्ति की,
   और स्वयं छह रूपों की व्युत्पत्ति करें।" (Ex 3, 6 items)
4. en "Say and write six sentences with ब्रवीति, हन्ति, एति and युनक्ति in strong and weak cells,
   joined, with the pause form beneath." · hi "ब्रवीति, हन्ति, एति और युनक्ति के सबल और निर्बल रूपों से
   छह वाक्य बोलें और लिखें।" (Ex 4, 6 items)

**The opening (§1).** v2.c04.l01 gave शृणोति; Volume 3's R4 card gave शृण्वन्ति, अशृणोत्, शृणु "learned
whole"; v3.c03.l01 gave अस्ति / सन्ति and करोति / कुर्वन्ति as whole words. Question: why शृणो- in one
cell and शृण्व- in the next? Why स्- in सन्ति and अस्- in अस्ति? And why no guṇa in लिखति, when नयति
has one?

**The one grammar point (plan).**
- A sārvadhātuka affix without the marker p counts as ṅ-marked (1.2.4), and before a k- or ṅ-marked
  affix guṇa and vṛddhi do not apply (1.1.5).
- So the singular P endings तिप् सिप् मिप्, which carry p, give a strong (guṇa) stem, and every other
  ending gives a weak one.
- The same marker explains why class 6 (श, with no p) has no guṇa at all.

**Forms taught.** The strong/weak table (below, every cell checked against Whitney); the laṅ and loṭ
split; E-g with the teacher; E-h by the learner; the anubandha card (K); the kṛt evidence table (○);
the IR-022 box.

**The strong/weak table** (laṭ P; the plan's, with the review's corrections c03:E#0, P#0, P#14):

| Verb | Before तिप् (pit → strong) | Before तस् (apit → ṅit → weak) | Before झि → -अन्ति (weak) | Rule beyond 1.2.4 + 1.1.5 | Whitney |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| शृणोति (sign श्नु; a bhvādi root, 3.1.74) | शृणोति | शृणुतः | शृण्वन्ति — उ + अ → व् by a rule in Teacher Notes (yaṇ), not uvaṅ | 3.1.74 श्रुवः शृ च (D) | §711, §698 |
| करोति (class 8) | करोति | कुरुतः | कुर्वन्ति | 6.4.110 अत उत् सार्वधातुके (D) (Kāśikā on 1.2.4: *kurutaḥ, kurvanti*) | §714 |
| अस्ति (class 2) | अस्ति | स्तः | सन्ति | 6.4.111 श्नसोरल्लोपः (D) | §636 |
| ब्रवीति (class 2) | ब्रवीति | ब्रूतः | ब्रुवन्ति | (the -ई- of ब्रवीति: Teacher Notes) | §632 |
| हन्ति (class 2) | हन्ति | हतः | घ्नन्ति | (Teacher Notes) | §637 |
| एति (class 2) | एति | इतः | यन्ति | (यन्ति: Teacher Notes) | §612 (class 2) |
| युनक्ति (class 7) | युनक्ति | युङ्क्तः | युञ्जन्ति | 3.1.78 (K); 6.4.111 (D) | §686 |
| गृह्णाति (class 9) | गृह्णाति | गृह्णीतः | गृह्णन्ति | 6.4.113 ई हल्यघोः (K) | §729, §717 |
| पृच्छति (class 6) | पृच्छति — श is apit, so ṅit: samprasāraṇa (no guṇa in any case: no final or light penultimate इक्) | पृच्छतः | पृच्छन्ति | 6.1.16 (D) | §752, §756 |

| Verb | 3sg · 3du · 3pl | 2sg · 2pl | 1sg · 1pl |
|---|---|---|---|
| शृणोति | शृणोति · शृणुतः · शृण्वन्ति | शृणोषि · शृणुथ | शृणोमि · शृणुमः (also शृण्मः, Teacher Notes) |

Cells checked: करोति (§714 table, quoted: करोमि … कुर्मस्, करोषि … कुरुथ, करोति कुरुतस् कुर्वन्ति), अस्ति
(§636a: अस्मि … स्मस्, असि … स्थ, अस्ति स्तस् सन्ति), ब्रवीति (§632: brávīmi, brávīṣi, brávīti … brūmás),

स्, STYLE_GUIDE §2), स्वपिति (§631: the union-vowel i before consonant endings except the -s of 2sg
impf.). **आप्नुवन्ति** (not *आप्न्वन्ति): after a conjunct the उ of नु takes uvaṅ (6.4.77; 6.4.87 needs
"asaṃyogapūrva", carried in its `an`, table 0.H note) — Teacher Notes; Pass C: Whitney §697 ff. for

in the table; **Pass C re-reads each cell against them** (§7 item 3). ददाति: its listed cells only

**laṅ and loṭ, the same split** (shown on शृणोति, the pattern for all; ✓ P):
- laṅ strong in the 3sg/2sg/1sg only: अशृणोत् · अशृणुताम् · अशृण्वन्; अशृणोः · अशृणुत; अशृणवम् · अशृणुम
  (also अशृण्म; LSK L7 prints *aśṛṇma, aśṛṇuma*).
- loṭ strong in the 3sg and in all the 1st persons: शृणोतु · शृणुताम् · शृण्वन्तु; शृणु · शृणुत; शृणवानि ·
  शृणवाम. (The 1st-person endings take the augment आट्, which is pit: Teacher Notes; the -नि of शृणवानि:
  Teacher Notes. The 2sg शृणु: its ending हि is apit, and then drops after the उ of नु — Teacher Notes;
  LSK VLk 505.) The 2sg imperatives शृणु, कुरु and देहि stay listed; गृहाण is a listed form of गृह्णाति
  (Teacher Notes: श्ना → आन before हि after a consonant, and हि dropped); N3b (Chapter 4 exam) has it.
- The same for करोति: अकरोत् · अकुरुताम् · अकुर्वन्; करोतु · कुरुताम् · कुर्वन्तु; कुरु · कुरुत; करवाणि ·
  करवाम (Whitney §714, the imperative and imperfect rows — Pass C).

**Sūtras by tier (tools added: 7).** Bracket lines: table 0.H.
- **Q — 1.2.4 सार्वधातुकमपित्** (*sārvadhātukam apit*). Padaccheda: सार्वधातुकम् (प्रथमा: "a
  sārvadhātuka affix") · अपित् (प्रथमा: "that is not p-marked") · [ङित्, carried from 1.2.1: "counts as
  ṅ-marked"]. English: "A sārvadhātuka affix that is not p-marked counts as ṅ-marked." [REF:kashika
  1.2.4] (K25: *kurutaḥ / kurvanti … apit iti kim? karoti*).
- **Q — 1.1.5 क्ङिति च** (*kṅiti ca*). Padaccheda: क्ङिति (सप्तमी: "before [an affix] with the marker क्
  or ङ्") · च ("and") · [इकः, गुण-वृद्धी, carried from 1.1.3; न "not", carried from 1.1.4]. English:
  "And before a k- or ṅ-marked affix, guṇa and vṛddhi [in place of an इक्] do not [apply]." [REF:kashika
  1.1.5] (K26: *kṅin-nimitte ye guṇa-vṛddhī prāpnutaḥ, te na bhavataḥ*).
- **D — 3.1.74 श्रुवः शृ च** — "For श्रु, [the class sign is] श्नु, and श्रु becomes शृ." [REF:kashika
  3.1.74] (K51; श्नुः carried from 3.1.73: श्रु is a bhvādi root — review c03:P#4, E#7).
- **D — 7.1.3 झोऽन्तः** — "The झ् of an affix is replaced by अन्त्." [REF:kashika 7.1.3] (K52).
- **D — 6.4.110 अत उत् सार्वधातुके** — "The अ of कृ [with its sign उ] becomes उ before a kṅit
  sārvadhātuka affix." [REF:kashika 6.4.110] (K49: *kurutaḥ / kurvanti … kṅiti ity eva, karoti*).
- **D — 6.4.111 श्नसोरल्लोपः** — "The अ of श्न, the न that श्नम् puts inside a class-7 root, and of अस् is dropped before a kṅit
  sārvadhātuka affix." Examples: युनक्ति → युङ्क्तः, अस्ति → सन्ति. Not the आ of the class-9 sign श्ना: गृह्णन्ति is 6.4.112 and गृह्णीतः is 6.4.113. The data file's padaccheda श्ना-असोः is corrected by the Kāśikā's *śnasya* (Teacher Notes). [REF:kashika 6.4.111] (K50: *rundhaḥ … asteḥ - staḥ / santi*).
- **D — 6.1.16 ग्रहिज्यावयिव्यधिवष्टिविचतिवृश्चतिपृच्छतिभृज्जतीनां ङिति च** — "Of ग्रह्, … प्रच्छ्, …,
  samprasāraṇa before a ṅit (and a kit) affix." [REF:kashika 6.1.16] (K52: *graha - gṛhītaḥ … ṅiti -
  gṛhṇāti*).
- **K — 6.4.113 ई हल्यघोः** — "[The आ of श्ना] becomes ई before a consonant-initial kṅit sārvadhātuka
  affix." (Kāśikā line 65521, Pass C adds it to table 0.E.)
- **Re-read (owned):** 7.3.84 (B), 3.1.68 (B), 3.4.113 (B), 8.4.1 (Q; the ण of शृणोति), 6.1.77 (owned;
  कुर्वन्ति).
- **Labelled previews:** the ṇ-marker's vṛddhi (c03.l04); the k-marker on kta (c04.l01); the ḍ-marker's
  ṭi-deletion (c03.l05 card, c04.l04); 3.4.114 (c03.l05; why the class-8 sign उ takes guṇa: E-h (1)).
- **Teacher Notes:** 6.4.87, 6.4.77, 7.3.93, 6.4.81, 6.4.37, 6.4.98, 7.3.54, 6.4.112, 1.1.47, 8.2.30,
  8.4.55, 6.1.108, 6.4.108 (कुर्मः), 6.4.107 (शृण्मः), 3.4.87, 6.4.105, 6.4.106, 3.4.89, 3.4.90, 3.4.91,
  3.4.92, 3.4.93, 3.1.83, 6.1.197, 3.1.4, 1.2.1, 1.1.4. (6.4.107 लोपश्चास्यान्यतरस्यां म्वोः and 6.4.108 नित्यं
  करोतेः, both found in the data file: the rules of शृण्मः and कुर्मः.)

**The derivations.** E-g with the teacher; E-h the learner's set.

**IR-022 corrected in the open (row 0.18; plain text, §8).** "The 1.x book's marker card said 'ङ् (ṅ)
— "no guṇa" (just guṇa block)' and, in its matching exercise, 'ङ् (ṅ) → Blocks guṇa' and 'क् (k) →
Blocks guṇa + vṛddhi' (pp. 93, 95). The rule makes no such difference: 1.1.5 क्ङिति च blocks guṇa and
vṛddhi alike before both markers — the Kāśikā: 'the guṇa and vṛddhi that would arise there do not
happen'. (The card's accent claim is corrected in Teacher Notes.)"

guṇa · ण्वुल् (ण्, ल्) कारकः vṛddhi (ṇ-marked: 7.2.115) · घञ् (घ्, ञ्) कारः vṛddhi. The affix names are

**Model sentences** (the plan's six; checked; seams per row 0.29).
1. वयं गुरोर्वचनं शृणुमः। (*vayaṃ guror vacanaṃ śṛṇumaḥ.*) — pause: वयम् गुरोः वचनम् शृणुमः। — "We
   listen to the teacher's words." — हम गुरु की बात सुनते हैं।
2. ते छात्राः सम्यक्कुर्वन्ति। (*te chātrāḥ samyak kurvanti.*) — pause: ते छात्राः सम्यक् कुर्वन्ति। —
   "Those students do it properly." — वे छात्र ठीक से करते हैं।
3. कुत्र स्तस्तौ? — गृहे स्तः। (*kutra stas tau? — gṛhe staḥ.*) — pause: कुत्र स्तः तौ? — गृहे स्तः। —
   "Where are those two? — At home." — वे दोनों कहाँ हैं? — घर पर हैं।
4. गुरुर्ब्रवीति, शिष्या ब्रुवन्ति। (*gurur bravīti, śiṣyā bruvanti.*) — pause: गुरुः ब्रवीति, शिष्याः
   ब्रुवन्ति। — "The teacher says; the students say." — गुरु बोलते हैं, शिष्य बोलते हैं।
5. व्याधो मृगान्हन्ति। (*vyādho mṛgān hanti.*) — pause: व्याधः मृगान् हन्ति। — "The hunter kills the
   deer." — शिकारी हिरणों को मारता है।
6. ते ग्रामं यन्ति। (*te grāmaṃ yanti.*) — pause: ते ग्रामम् यन्ति। — "They go to the village." — वे
   गाँव जाते हैं।
A seventh line for युनक्ति (no plan sentence uses it; §8, deviation 4): नरो रथं युनक्ति। (*naro rathaṃ
yunakti.*) — pause: नरः रथम् युनक्ति। — "The man yokes the chariot." — आदमी रथ जोतता है। (नर V1, रथः

**§3 Station 0 (verbatim) and review prompts.**
- +1 day: ten verbs classified — for गच्छति, नृत्यति, पृच्छति, आप्नोति, करोति, गृह्णाति, कथयति, याति,
  ददाति and मन्यते name the class (by its first root), the class sign and the present stem;
  v4.c03.l03's Station 0 runs exactly this.
- +1 week: पठति to step 7 — derive, according to Pāṇini, in table form, पठ → पठ् + ति (steps 0–7 of the
  पठति table), each step with its sūtra; v4.c03.l03's Station 0 runs exactly this.
Keys: S0-ec, S0-eb. Review prompts: (v3.c03.l01) "Say 'they are' and 'they do'" — सन्ति; कुर्वन्ति;
(v3.c03.l03) "Say 'he heard' and 'he took' (R4 imperfects)" — अशृणोत्; अगृह्णात्; (v3.c03.l04) "Say
'listen!' to one person" — शृणु; (v2.c04.l01) "Say 'he hears' and 'he knows'" — शृणोति; जानाति.

**§4 Historical Context.** **[TRADITION · परम्परा]** "The Kāśikā gives, under 1.2.4, the very forms of
this lesson: कुरुतः, कुर्वन्ति beside करोति — 'why "not p-marked"? karoti' — that is, करोति keeps its
guṇa because तिप् has a p. The commentary's examples are the course's derivations. [REF:kashika 1.2.4]"

**§5 Scientific Explanation (FACT).** Row 0.17: Whitney §604 ("a shift of accent: the tone being now
upon the ending, and now upon the root or the class-sign") and §612; the two accounts pick out the same
strong cells (the singular active) and explain them differently (accent vs the p-marker). Both print.

**§6 Sanskrit Explanation.** Model sentences 1, 3, and: तिपि पकारोऽस्ति, तसि नास्ति। (*tipi pakāro 'sti, tasi nāsti.*) — pause: तिपि पकारः अस्ति, तसि न अस्ति। — "In tip there is a p; in tas there is none." (labels in the locative, as शपि and झौ in §16; पकारोऽस्ति:
-अः + अ → ओऽ.)

**§7 Hindi Explanation.** Natively written: पित् और अपित् प्रत्यय; अपित् सार्वधातुक ङित् जैसा (1.2.4); ङित्
से पहले गुण-वृद्धि नहीं (1.1.5); शृणोति/शृण्वन्ति, करोति/कुर्वन्ति. Terms: पित्, ङित्, सबल अङ्ग, निर्बल अङ्ग.

**§8 English Explanation.** The recipe: (1) शृणोति / शृण्वन्ति, अस्ति / सन्ति; (2) why two stems? (3) the
tools: 1.2.4, 1.1.5; (4) E-g; (5) E-h; (6) sentences. The IR-022 box; the anubandha card; the kṛt
evidence table as display; the main-risk fix (thematic verbs have no split: a weak *भुवतः does not
exist — the class sign अ takes the endings; review c03:P#10: no correct form is starred).

**§9–§11.** Row 0.36: शृणोति /ɕr̩ɳoːt̪i/ vs शृण्वन्ति /ɕr̩ɳʋɐn̪t̪i/; ब्रवीति /bɾɐʋiːt̪i/ vs ब्रूतः /bɾuːt̪ɐh/;
युङ्क्तः /juŋkt̪ɐh/ (the ङ् heard before क्); घ्नन्ति /gʱn̪ɐn̪t̪i/.

**§12 Common mistakes.**

| # | Mistake | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Applying strong/weak to thematic verbs (a weak *भुवतः for भवतः) | thematic classes (1, 4, 6, 10) have no split: the sign अ takes the endings (two-column sort) |
| 2 | "ङ् blocks guṇa; क् blocks guṇa and vṛddhi" (the 1.x card) | 1.1.5 blocks both before both |
| 3 | *शृणोन्ति, *करोन्ति | झि has no p: ṅit (1.2.4) → no guṇa (1.1.5): शृण्वन्ति, कुर्वन्ति |
| 4 | *अस्न्ति or *असन्ति | 6.4.111 drops the अ of अस् before a kṅit ending: सन्ति, स्तः |
| 5 | *प्रच्छति, with the root unchanged | श has no p: ṅit (1.2.4); 6.1.16 gives samprasāraṇa before a ṅit affix: पृच्छ-. (पृच्छ- has no guṇa because its ऋ is neither final nor penultimate. The class-6 ṅit that blocks guṇa shows in लिखति, not *लेखति.) |
| 6 | Reading the weak cells as "irregular" | one rule pair (1.2.4 + 1.1.5) and one rule per verb (6.4.110, 6.4.111 …) |

card; the kṛt evidence table (○).

**§15 Grammar.** The rule pair (1.2.4 + 1.1.5); the strong/weak table and the full laṭ cells; the laṅ
and loṭ split; the anubandha card (K); the kṛt table (○). **Toolbox (cumulative 75 = 68 + 7).**

cells; ◐ the class-3 cells beyond them, the athematic Ā cells beyond the listed (ब्रूते, कुरुते, coded L
in the readings). R4 closes.

**§16 Conversation (10 turns; homework).**
1. छात्रा — गुरो, शृणोतीत्यत्र गुणः कस्मात्? (*guro, śṛṇotīty atra guṇaḥ kasmāt?*) — pause: गुरो, शृणोति
   इति अत्र गुणः कस्मात्? — "Teacher, why guṇa in śṛṇoti?" (कस्मात्: abl. of किम्, ✓.)
2. गुरुः — तिपः पकारात्। (*tipaḥ pakārāt.*) — pause: तिपः पकारात्। — "Because of the p of tip."
3. छात्रा — शृण्वन्तीत्यत्र किम्? (*śṛṇvantīty atra kim?*) — pause: शृण्वन्ति इति अत्र किम्? — "And in
   śṛṇvanti?"
4. गुरुः — झौ पकारो नास्ति, अतो गुणो न भवति। (*jhau pakāro nāsti, ato guṇo na bhavati.*) — pause: झौ
   पकारः न अस्ति, अतः गुणः न भवति। — "In jhi there is no p, so there is no guṇa."
5. छात्रा — सन्तीत्यत्राकारः कुत्र? (*santīty atrākāraḥ kutra?*) — pause: सन्ति इति अत्र अकारः कुत्र? —
   "In santi, where is the a?"
6. गुरुः — लोपेन गतः। (*lopena gataḥ.*) — pause: the same — "Gone, by deletion."
7. छात्रा — लिखतीत्यत्र गुणो नास्ति? (*likhatīty atra guṇo nāsti?*) — pause: लिखति इति अत्र गुणः न
   अस्ति? — "In likhati there is no guṇa?"
8. गुरुः — नास्ति। शे पकारो न। (*nāsti. śe pakāro na.*) — pause: न अस्ति। शे पकारः न। — "No. The śa has
   no p."
9. छात्रा — एवं भवतीत्यत्रापि? (*evaṃ bhavatīty atrāpi?*) — pause: एवम् भवति इति अत्र अपि? — "Is it so
   in bhavati too?"
10. गुरुः — न, शपि पकारोऽस्ति। (*na, śapi pakāro 'sti.*) — pause: न, शपि पकारः अस्ति। — "No: śap has
    a p."
Words owned: गुरुः, गुणः, किम् (कस्मात्), लोपः (c02.l02; लोपेन ✓), गतः (V1), अस्ति, न, अतः, एवम्, कुत्र,
अपि; labels: पकारः, अकारः, तिप् (तिपः gen.), झि (झौ loc. of the label, ○), श (शे), शप् (शपि). Seams:
शृणोतीत्यत्र, पकारो नास्ति (-अः + न → ओ; न + अ → ना), अतो गुणो न, सन्तीत्यत्राकारः (अ + अ → आ),
लिखतीत्यत्र, गुणो नास्ति, पकारो न, भवतीत्यत्रापि, पकारोऽस्ति (-अः + अ → ओऽ). None of the exam's items.

**§17 Story — the serial, c03 episode 3: the wife's suspicion, in the present only (our retelling).**
1. मकरः सदा वानरस्य समीपे तिष्ठति। (*makaraḥ sadā vānarasya samīpe tiṣṭhati.*) — pause: the same —
   "The crocodile always stays near the monkey." (समीपम् owned v3.c01.l05.)
2. पत्नी गृहे दुःखेन तिष्ठति, चिन्तयति च। (*patnī gṛhe duḥkhena tiṣṭhati, cintayati ca.*) — pause: the
   same — "The wife stays at home in sorrow, and thinks."
3. "तस्य वानर्यस्ति" इति सा ब्रवीति। (*"tasya vānary asti" iti sā bravīti.*) — pause: "तस्य वानरी
   अस्ति" इति सा ब्रवीति। — "'He has a she-monkey,' she says." (the plan's line, joined at review
   c03:P#2: ई + अ → य)
4. "अतः स तत्र गच्छति, न गृहम्।" (*"ataḥ sa tatra gacchati, na gṛham."*) — pause: "अतः सः तत्र गच्छति, न गृहम्।" — "'That is why he goes there, and not home.'"
5. मकरो गृहमेति, पत्नी तु किमपि न ब्रवीति। (*makaro gṛham
   eti, patnī tu kim api na bravīti.*) — pause: मकरः गृहम् एति, पत्नी तु किम् अपि न ब्रवीति। — "The
   crocodile comes home, but the wife says nothing."
Gloss: वानरी "she-monkey" (the feminine of वानरः, L). Words owned: समीपम्, दुःखम्, चिन्तयति, सा, अतः, सः,
तत्र, गच्छति, न, गृहम्, तु, किम् अपि; today's ब्रवीति, एति. Seams: वानर्यस्ति (ई + अ → य; no sandhi across
the closing quotation mark before इति: "…अस्ति" इति kept apart, STYLE_GUIDE §3.1), "अतः स तत्र" (the
quotation opens: अतः kept; सः → स before त), गृहमेति, किमपि. (अतः सः → अतः स: सः before a consonant, 6.1.132 owned; the quotation opens with अतः.)

**§18 Exercises.**
- **Ex 1 (obj 1; class 5 min)** — "Pit or not? Strong or weak stem before it?" (1) तिप् (2) तस् (3) झि
  (4) सिप् (5) थस् (6) थ (7) मिप् (8) वस् (9) मस् (10) the Ā ending त (11) the class sign श (12) the class
  sign शप्. Key: pit (strong): तिप्, सिप्, मिप् (and शप् is pit, so a root before it takes guṇa: नयति);
  not pit (ṅit, weak): तस्, झि, थस्, थ, वस्, मस्, त, श. One point each. (12)'s key adds: "the root before
  शप् is strong: guṇa (नयति)".
- **Ex 2 (obj 2; class 6 min)** — "Fill the cell": (1) अस् 3pl laṭ (2) कृ 3du laṭ (3) श्रु 3pl laṭ (4) ब्रू
  3du laṭ (5) हन् 3pl laṭ (6) इ 3pl laṭ (7) युज् 3du laṭ (8) ग्रह् 3du laṭ (9) श्रु 3sg laṅ (10) कृ 3pl laṅ
  (11) श्रु 2sg loṭ (12) कृ 1sg loṭ. Key: (1) सन्ति (2) कुरुतः (3) शृण्वन्ति (4) ब्रूतः (5) घ्नन्ति (6)
  यन्ति (7) युङ्क्तः (8) गृह्णीतः (9) अशृणोत् (10) अकुर्वन् (11) शृणु (12) करवाणि. One point each.
- **Ex 3 (obj 3; derive it yourself; homework 12 min)** — "Derive, according to Pāṇini, in table form":
  (1) कृ → करोति (2) कृ → कुरुतः (3) कृ → कुर्वन्ति (4) अस् → अस्ति (5) अस् → सन्ति (6) प्रच्छ् → पृच्छति.
  Key: E-h (1)–(6).
- **Ex 4 (obj 4; production; homework 8 min)** — "In Sanskrit, joined, with the pause form": (1) "The
  teacher says this." (2) "The students say this." (3) "The hunters kill the deer." (4) "They go to the
  forest." (5) "We listen to the teacher." (6) "The man yokes the chariot." Key: (1) गुरुरेतद्ब्रवीति। — pause: गुरुः एतत् ब्रवीति। (2) छात्रा
  एतद्ब्रुवन्ति। — pause: छात्राः एतत् ब्रुवन्ति। (3) व्याधा मृगान्घ्नन्ति। — pause: व्याधाः मृगान्
  घ्नन्ति। (4) ते वनं यन्ति। — pause: ते वनम् यन्ति। (5) वयं गुरुं शृणुमः। — pause: वयम् गुरुम्
  शृणुमः। (6) नरो रथं युनक्ति। — pause: नरः रथम् युनक्ति। Accept शृण्मः in (5).
- **Ex 5 (the sort; class 3 min)** — "Thematic or athematic? Does it have a strong/weak split?" भवति,
  शृणोति, नृत्यति, अस्ति, पृच्छति, करोति, कथयति, एति. Key: thematic, no split — भवति, नृत्यति, पृच्छति,
  कथयति; athematic, split — शृणोति, अस्ति, करोति, एति. (Serves obj 1.)
- **Ex 6 (§9–§11; homework 2 min)** — "Read शृणोति / शृण्वन्ति aloud with the table, naming the markers."

**§19 Quiz (10; homework).**
1. According to Pāṇini (1.2.4), an apit sārvadhātuka affix counts as: (a) ṅ-marked (b) p-marked (c)
   ārdhadhātuka (d) a taddhita — a. TRADITION
2. According to Pāṇini (1.1.5), before a ṅ-marked affix: (a) neither guṇa nor vṛddhi (b) only vṛddhi
   is blocked (c) only guṇa is blocked (d) guṇa is required — a. TRADITION (b, c: the 1.x card)
3. Which ending gives a strong stem? (a) तिप् (b) तस् (c) झि (d) थस् — a. TRADITION
4. The 3pl present of अस् is: (a) सन्ति (b) स्तः (c) अस्ति (d) स्थ — a. FACT [REF:whitney1889 §636]
   (no non-form stands as an option: verification log, "an untaught form may not stand even as a wrong
   option")
5. The 3du present of कृ is: (a) कुरुतः (b) करोति (c) कुर्वन्ति (d) करोषि — a. FACT [REF:whitney1889
   §714]
6. According to Pāṇini, the अ of कर्- becomes उ in कुरुतः by: (a) 6.4.110 (b) 6.4.111 (c) 3.1.74 (d)
   7.3.84 — a. TRADITION
7. Why is there no guṇa in लिखति, when नयति has one, according to Pāṇini? (a) its sign श has no p, so it is ṅit (b) its
   root has no इक् (c) it is class 1 (d) the ending is Ā — a. TRADITION
8. शृणोति's sign is श्नु, yet श्रु is listed among the bhvādi roots, because: (a) 3.1.74 gives it श्नु in
   place of शप् (b) it is a class-5 root (c) it is irregular (d) it has no sign — a. TRADITION
9. The 3pl present of हन् is: (a) घ्नन्ति (b) हन्ति (c) हतः (d) हंसि — a. FACT [REF:whitney1889 §637]
10. Whitney ties the strong and weak stems to: (a) a shift of accent (b) the marker p (c) the class
    sign श (d) the dual — a. FACT [REF:whitney1889 §604]


**§20 bookings (verbatim).**
- +1 day: eight strong and weak cells — write the 3rd singular and the 3rd plural present of शृणोति,
  करोति, अस्ति and एति, and say for each, according to Pāṇini, whether its ending is pit (strong stem) or
  not (weak stem); v4.c03.l04's Station 0 runs exactly this.
- +1 week: the anubandha card — say what each of these markers does and name its rule: the ट् of लट्,
  the ञ् of डुकृञ्, the श् of शप्, the प् of तिप्, the missing प् of तस्, the ण् of णल्; v4.c03.l05's
  Station 0 runs exactly this.

with the teacher (6) · 22–27 Ex 1 (5) · 27–30 Ex 5, the sort (3) · 30–34 the strong/weak table read, the
D rules 6.4.110, 6.4.111, 6.1.16, 3.1.74 (4) · 34–40 Ex 2 (6) · 40–43 the laṅ and loṭ split shown on शृणोति and करोति (3; the anubandha card is read at home with §15) · 43–45
model sentences 1–6 aloud (2). Homework: Ex 3, Ex 4, Ex 6, the kṛt table read, §16, §17, the quiz.

## 4. L4 — लिट्: The Perfect (grammar)

(गम् copied twice; the first copy trimmed to ग, turned to ज; the second's अ lengthened; the ṇ of णल्
pointing at the vowel); audio `v4c03l04-derivation-read.mp3`, `v4c03l04-n1-read.mp3`,

**Objectives.**
1. en "Write the 3rd-person perfect (singular, dual, plural) of eight owned verbs from the production
   table." · hi "उत्पादन-तालिका से आठ परिचित क्रियाओं के लिट् प्रथम पुरुष (एक, द्वि, बहुवचन) लिखें।" (Ex 1,
   8 items)
2. en "Derive, according to Pāṇini, जगाम with the teacher, and ददौ, पपाठ, पपाच, पपौ, चचार and ययौ by
   yourself." · hi "पाणिनि के अनुसार शिक्षक के साथ जगाम की, और स्वयं छह लिट् रूपों की व्युत्पत्ति करें।"
   (Ex 3, 6 items)
3. en "Read the Hitopadeśa passage of the lesson and answer six questions, naming the root of each
   perfect." · hi "पाठ का हितोपदेश-अंश पढ़ें और छह प्रश्नों के उत्तर दें, हर लिट् रूप का धातु बताते हुए।"
   (Ex 4, 6 items)
4. en "Tell an episode in six sentences with 3rd-person perfects, joined, with the pause form
   beneath." · hi "लिट् प्रथम पुरुष के रूपों से छह वाक्यों में एक प्रसंग कहें और लिखें।" (Ex 5, 6 items)

**The opening (§1).** Volume 3 gave उवाच and आह as whole words (v3.c03.l03) and glossed ददौ in the
Pañcatantra (v3.c06.l03). Question: what kind of form is उवाच, with its first syllable उ- that वच् does
not have — and why do जगाम and ददौ begin with a second copy of their root's first sound?

**The one grammar point (plan).**
- The perfect (लिट्) is made by doubling the root (6.1.8) and adding its own endings (3.4.82).
- The singular active is strong (vṛddhi or guṇa). Its endings णल्, थल् (and the 1sg णल्) carry no प्:
  they count as pit only because they replace तिप्, सिप्, मिप्, and a substitute behaves like what it
  replaced (1.1.56, previewed for c06.l03). The other endings, after a root not ending in a conjunct,
  are kit by 1.2.5 असंयोगाल्लिट् कित् (K), so they take the weak root.
- According to Pāṇini (3.2.115) it is the past "not witnessed by the speaker" (परोक्षे). FACT beside it
  (row 0.20).

**Forms taught (T10; Whitney §553c, §§793–797, 800).**

| | 3rd | 2nd | 1st |
|---|---|---|---|
| P endings (3.4.82) | णल् -अ · अतुस् -अतुः · उस् -उः | थल् -थ · अथुस् -अथुः · अ -अ | णल् -अ · व -व · म -म |

(Whitney §553c, perfect endings, active: 1 `a vá má`, 2 `tha áthus á`, 3 `a átus ús` — the same nine;
the course prints the visarga.) **Memorised: T10** (plan §1.3.2).

**Production cells (✓, 3rd person P):**

| Root (owned verb) | 3sg | 3du | 3pl | How (sūtras: D taught, K on the card, Teacher Notes) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| गम् (गच्छति) | जगाम | जग्मतुः | जग्मुः | doubling (6.1.8), ग → ज (7.4.62), upadhā vṛddhi before णल् (7.2.116); weak: the अ dropped before a vowel-initial kit ending (Teacher Notes; Kāśikā's own examples *jagmatuḥ, jagmuḥ*) |
| कृ (करोति) | चकार | चक्रतुः | चक्रुः | doubling; ऋ → अ in the doubled syllable (7.4.66, K); क → च (7.4.62); vṛddhi (7.2.115, K) |
| भू (भवति) | बभूव | बभूवतुः | बभूवुः | no strong singular (*बभाव): 1.2.6 makes liṭ after भू k-marked (K; Kāśikā: *bhavateḥ khalv api — babhūva*); the अ and ब of the doubled syllable and the व: Teacher Notes; the prakriyā is read in the LSK, c06.l02 |
| दा (ददाति) | ददौ | ददतुः | ददुः | -औ for णल् after आ (7.1.34); weak: the आ dropped before a vowel-initial kit ending (Teacher Notes) |
| वच् (उवाच) | उवाच | ऊचतुः | ऊचुः | doubled syllable va → u (6.1.17, K); weak: वच् → उच् (6.1.15, 1.2.5, K; "u + a → u", a labelled step) |
| पत् (पतति) | पपात | पेततुः | पेतुः | weak पेत्- (Teacher Notes; Kāśikā's pattern *pecatuḥ*) |
| श्रु (शृणोति) | शुश्राव | शुश्रुवतुः | शुश्रुवुः | doubling; vṛddhi of उ (7.2.115, K) → औ → आव् (6.1.78); weak: उ → उव् before a vowel (Teacher Notes) |
| ब्रू → **आह** (ब्रवीति) | आह | आहतुः | आहुः | 3.4.84: a substitute for 5 forms of ब्रू's laṭ (K; below) |

Cells checked: Whitney §800 (the paradigm examples; Pass C prints the line for each of जगाम, चकार,
ददौ, उवाच, पपात, शुश्राव), §793b (बभूव), §801a (आह, āhatus, āhús), §794c (the weak grades), and the
Kāśikā's examples (K28–K35, K52; 6.4.98 *jagmatuḥ, jagmuḥ*; 6.4.64 *papatuḥ, papuḥ*). The 3rd-person
perfect of every owned verb is printed in its vocabulary row from today (plan): पठति पपाठ · पचति पपाच ·
चरति चचार · वदति उवाद (◐ pattern: 6.1.15–17) · पिबति पपौ · याति ययौ · नयति निनाय · पश्यति ददर्श ·
तिष्ठति तस्थौ · लभते लेभे (Ā ◐) · जयति जिगाय · हरति जहार · हन्ति जघान · प्राप्नोति प्राप (Pass C: each
against Apte's root entry, which prints the perfect: e.g. Apte s.v. pac "papāca-pece", s.v. hṛ

periphrastic perfect (चिन्तयामास, 3.1.35 + 3.1.40, K).

**TRADITION and FACT side by side on आह** (row 0.20; three statements printed; only "According to
Pāṇini…" assessed; review c03:E#1, P#5: no "the Hitopadeśa uses आह as a past").

**Sūtras by tier (tools added: 8).** Bracket lines: table 0.H.
- **Q — 3.2.115 परोक्षे लिट्** (*parokṣe liṭ*). Padaccheda: परोक्षे (सप्तमी: "in the sense of 'out of
  sight'" — a locative of meaning) · लिट् (प्रथमा: the affix) · [अनद्यतने, carried from 3.2.111; भूते,
  the heading 3.2.84, by name]. English: "[After a root, for the past not of today,] liṭ, when it is out
  of sight." [REF:kashika 3.2.115] (K27: *bhūtānadyatanaparokṣe*).
- **D — 6.1.8 लिटि धातोरनभ्यासस्य** — "Before liṭ, [the first part, with its one vowel,] of a root that
  is not already doubled is doubled." [REF:kashika 6.1.8] (K28).
- **D — 3.4.82 परस्मैपदानां णलतुसुस्थलथुसणल्वमाः** — "In liṭ, the nine P endings are replaced by णल्,
  अतुस्, उस्, थल्, अथुस्, अ, णल्, व, म." [REF:kashika 3.4.82] (K29: *ṇakāro vṛddhy-arthaḥ*).
- **D — 7.2.116 अत उपधायाः** — "Before a ñ- or ṇ-marked affix, a penultimate अ is replaced by vṛddhi
  (आ)." [REF:kashika 7.2.116] (K30).
- **D — 7.4.60 हलादिः शेषः** — "In the doubled syllable, the first consonant remains; the others go."
  [REF:kashika 7.4.60] (K31).
- **D — 7.4.59 ह्रस्वः** — "The vowel of the doubled syllable is shortened." [REF:kashika 7.4.59] (K32).
- **D — 7.4.62 कुहोश्चुः** — "In the doubled syllable, a k-row sound or ह becomes a c-row sound."
  [REF:kashika 7.4.62] (K33: *cakāra … jagāma*).
- **D — 7.1.34 आत औ णलः** — "After an आ-final aṅga, णल् is replaced by औ." [REF:kashika 7.1.34] (K34).
- **K (the card "the perfect's further tools", read with the production table, not derived):** 7.4.66
  उरत् ("In the doubled syllable, ऋ becomes अ"); 8.4.54 अभ्यासे चर्च ("In the doubled syllable, a jhal
  sound becomes car (or jaś)" — the ब of बभूव); 7.2.115 अचो ञ्णिति ("vṛddhi of a final vowel before a ñ-
  or ṇ-marked affix"); 6.1.17 लिट्यभ्यासस्योभयेषाम् ("before liṭ, samprasāraṇa of the doubled syllable of
  both groups": उवाच); 6.1.15 वचिस्वपियजादीनां किति ("samprasāraṇa of वच्, स्वप् and the yajādi before a
  kit affix"); 1.2.5 असंयोगाल्लिट् कित् ("liṭ after a root not ending in a conjunct, apit, counts as
  k-marked"); 1.2.6 इन्धिभवतिभ्यां च ("and after इन्ध् and भू", K52: *bhavateḥ … pidartham* — "for the pit
  endings"); 3.4.84 ब्रुवः पञ्चानामादित आहो ब्रुवः; 3.1.35 कास्प्रत्ययादाममन्त्रे लिटि; 3.1.40 कृञ्
  चानुप्रयुज्यते लिटि ("the periphrastic perfect: आम् + कृ (or भू, अस्)", Kāśikā line 14639–14640:

**IR-019 corrected in the open (row 0.21; plain text).** "The 1.x book's recital card labelled लिट्
'perfect (remote, witnessed)' (p. 75), and its own question on the same page — 'Which is for events the
speaker did NOT witness?' — gave लिट् as the answer. The sūtra says परोक्षे, 'out of sight', and the
Kāśikā glosses its domain as the past, not of today, and out of sight. What the language does is
another matter (below)."

production table, not written as a derivation (review c03:E#18).

131). Locus (b): Hit 3 (Vigraha), story 8 (Vīravara), prose after Hit 3.101 (lines 2946, 2956), with an
ellipsis between. E-text, IAST, character by character:
> (a) *asti bhāgīrathī-tīre pāṭaliputra-nāmadheyaṃ nagaram | tatra sarva-svāmi-guṇopetaḥ sudarśano nāma
> narapatir āsīt | sa bhūpatir ekadā kenāpi pāṭhyamānaṃ śloka-dvayaṃ śuśrāva-*
> (b) *athaikadā kṛṣṇa-caturdaśyāṃ rātrau sa rājā sa-karuṇa-krandana-dhvaniṃ śuśrāva | tat śrutvā rājā
> brūte-kaḥ ko 'tra dvāri tiṣṭhati ?* … *tato rājāpi khaḍgam ādāya tad-anusaraṇa-krameṇa nagarād bahir
> nirjagāma |*

Printed joined (the course's Devanagari; changes listed for Teacher Notes: the hyphens of compounds
dropped; *tat śrutvā* joined तच्छ्रुत्वा; the e-text's final dash after *śuśrāva* (introducing the
verses) replaced by a daṇḍa; the dash after *brūte* is the speech frame):
> (a) अस्ति भागीरथीतीरे पाटलिपुत्रनामधेयं नगरम्। तत्र सर्वस्वामिगुणोपेतः सुदर्शनो नाम नरपतिरासीत्। स
> भूपतिरेकदा केनापि पाठ्यमानं श्लोकद्वयं शुश्राव।
> (b) अथैकदा कृष्णचतुर्दश्यां रात्रौ स राजा सकरुणक्रन्दनध्वनिं शुश्राव। तच्छ्रुत्वा राजा ब्रूते — कः
> कोऽत्र द्वारि तिष्ठति? … ततो राजापि खड्गमादाय तदनुसरणक्रमेण नगराद्बहिर्निर्जगाम।

**Model sentences** (the plan's six; checked; seams per row 0.29).
1. पुरा नलो नाम राजा बभूव। (*purā nalo nāma rājā babhūva.*) — pause: पुरा नलः नाम राजा बभूव। — "Long
   ago there was a king named Nala." — बहुत पहले नल नाम के एक राजा थे। (Nala a display name, rule 12)
2. स राजा नगराद्बहिर्जगाम। (*sa rājā nagarād bahir jagāma.*) — pause: सः राजा नगरात् बहिः जगाम। — "That
   king went out of the city." — वह राजा नगर से बाहर गया।
3. ऋषिः शिष्यानुवाच। (*ṛṣiḥ śiṣyān uvāca.*) — pause: ऋषिः शिष्यान् उवाच। — "The seer spoke to his
   pupils." — ऋषि ने शिष्यों से कहा।
4. तस्मै धनं ददौ। (*tasmai dhanaṃ dadau.*) — pause: तस्मै धनम् ददौ। — "He gave him wealth." — उसने उसे
   धन दिया।
5. ते सर्वे श्लोकं शुश्रुवुः। (*te sarve ślokaṃ śuśruvuḥ.*) — pause: ते सर्वे श्लोकम् शुश्रुवुः। — "They
   all heard the verse." — उन सबने श्लोक सुना।
6. पतिः खड्गेन सह द्वारं जगाम। (*patiḥ khaḍgena saha dvāraṃ jagāma.*) — pause: पतिः खड्गेन सह द्वारम्
   जगाम। — "The husband went to the door with a sword." — पति तलवार लेकर द्वार पर गया।

**§3 Station 0 (verbatim) and review prompts.**
- +1 day: eight strong and weak cells — write the 3rd singular and the 3rd plural present of शृणोति,
  करोति, अस्ति and एति, and say for each, according to Pāṇini, whether its ending is pit (strong stem) or
  not (weak stem); v4.c03.l04's Station 0 runs exactly this.
- +1 week: नयति unaided — derive, according to Pāṇini, in table form, नी → नयति, every step with its
  sūtra, from memory; v4.c03.l04's Station 0 runs exactly this.
Keys: S0-ee, S0-ed. Review prompts: (v3.c03.l03) "Say 'he said' three ways" — उवाच, अब्रवीत्, आह;
(v3.c06.l02) "In the doves' story, 'चित्रग्रीव उवाच' — who speaks, and what does उवाच mean?" —
Citragrīva, the king of the doves; "(he) said" (the lesson's speech frame); (v3.c06.l03) "What did the
monkey do with the fruits? (ददौ)" — he gave them (तस्मै जम्बूफलानि ददौ).

**§4 Historical Context.** **[TRADITION · परम्परा]** row 0.20's TRADITION and FACT side by side, as one
callout pair: "[TRADITION] According to Pāṇini, liṭ is the past 'out of sight' (3.2.115); the Kāśikā:
'the past, not of today, and out of sight'. [REF:kashika 3.2.115]" · "[FACT] Whitney: 'there is no
evidence of its being either exclusively or distinctively so employed at any period'; in the later
language 'it is simply a preterit or past tense, equivalent with the imperfect … on the whole less
common than the imperfect, although the preferences of different authors are diverse'.
[REF:whitney1889 §821]"

**§5 Scientific Explanation (FACT).** Whitney §781 (the four characteristics of the perfect: "a stem
made by reduplication of the root; a distinction between stronger and weaker forms of stem … endings in
some respects peculiar … the frequent use … of a union-vowel इ"); the three statements on आह (row 0.20);
the DCS line (row 0.27) printed only when the `dcs` key exists, else Teacher Notes.

**§6 Sanskrit Explanation.** Model sentences 1, 2, and: जगामेति लिट्, गमिति धातुः। (*jagāmeti liṭ, gam iti dhātuḥ.*) — pause: जगाम
इति लिट्, गम् इति धातुः। — "jagāma is liṭ; the root is gam." (labels; जगामेति: अ + इ → ए.)

**§7 Hindi Explanation.** Natively written: लिट् लकार: धातु का द्वित्व (अभ्यास) और अपने प्रत्यय; प्रथम
पुरुष के रूप; परोक्ष (पाणिनि) और वास्तविक प्रयोग (ह्विटनी). Terms: लिट्, अभ्यास, द्वित्व, परोक्ष.

**§8 English Explanation.** The recipe: (1) उवाच, ददौ; (2) why the doubled first syllable? (3) the tools:
6.1.8, 3.4.82, 7.4.59–62, 7.2.116, 7.1.34; (4) E-i; (5) E-j, E-k; (6) narration with 3rd-person perfects.
The IR-019 box; the TRADITION/FACT callout; the आह triplet; the main-risk line: "the perfect is the
narrator's tense here: 3rd person. Your 'I went' is the imperfect अगच्छम्, which you own."

**§9–§11.** Row 0.36: E-i read aloud ("णल् — the ण् and ल् are markers; what you hear is अ"); जगाम
/d͡ʑɐgɑːmɐ/, जग्मुः /d͡ʑɐgmuh/, ददौ /d̪ɐd̪ɐu̯/, बभूव /bɐbʱuːʋɐ/, उवाच /uʋɑːt͡ɕɐ/, शुश्राव /ɕuɕɾɑːʋɐ/; N1
read aloud joined.

**§12 Common mistakes.**

| # | Mistake | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Reaching for the perfect in the 1st person ("I went": जगाम) | the 1st-person perfect is ◐ here; "I went" is अगच्छम् (owned) |
| 2 | *बभाव for बभूव | भू's liṭ is k-marked even before णल् (1.2.6, K): no vṛddhi |
| 3 | *गगाम | the doubled ग becomes ज (7.4.62) |
| 4 | Doubling before 7.1.34 in ददौ and then getting *दादौ | the Kāśikā's order: औ first, then one vowel for आ + औ, then doubling, then the short vowel (7.4.59) |
| 5 | "लिट् = remote past, witnessed" (the 1.x card) | परोक्षे: "out of sight" (3.2.115); in use, a past tense (Whitney §821) |
| 6 | Saying "the Hitopadeśa uses आह as a past" | three statements: Pāṇini (a laṭ substitute, 3.4.84), Whitney (a perfect of √अह्, with present value), the DCS (a tag of the form) |

line and inventory); the Kāśikā's examples (पपाच, पपाठ, जगाम, चकार, पपौ).

(K card); E-i; E-l. **Toolbox (cumulative 83 = 75 + 8).** Paradigm ledger: ✓ the perfect, 3rd persons
P, of the production verbs and of every owned verb whose vocabulary row prints it; ◐ 1st/2nd persons,
Ā, periphrastic.

**Previewed / Deferred (plan).** Previewed: the perfect participle (विद्वान्, c05.l03, ◐); बभूव's
derivation (c06.l02). Deferred: 1st/2nd persons and Ā (◐ through Volume 5); the periphrastic perfect in
full (Volume 5 reading). **Payoff (plan):** उवाच, आह and the glossed ददौ of Volume 3 become cells; the ṇ
of णल् does the same work as the ṇ of णिच् (c05.l02) and the ñ of the ञ-taddhitas (c04.l03); the vṛddhi
row of the anubandha card now has its first taught marker.

**§16 Conversation (8 turns; homework).**
1. छात्रः — गुरो, उवाचेत्यत्र को धातुः? (*guro, uvācety atra ko dhātuḥ?*) — pause: गुरो, उवाच इति अत्र कः धातुः? — "Teacher, in uvāca, what is the
   root?"
2. गुरुः — वचेति धातुः, लिडिति लकारः। (*vaceti dhātuḥ, liḍ iti lakāraḥ.*) — pause: वच इति धातुः, लिट्
   इति लकारः। — "The root is vac, the l-affix liṭ." (लिडिति: ट् → ड् before a vowel.)
3. छात्रः — जगामेत्यत्र गकारः कुत्र? (*jagāmety atra gakāraḥ kutra?*) — pause: जगाम इति अत्र गकारः
   कुत्र? — "In jagāma, where is the g?"
4. गुरुः — अभ्यासे जकारो भवति। (*abhyāse jakāro bhavati.*) — pause: अभ्यासे जकारः भवति। — "In the
   doubled syllable it becomes j."
5. छात्रः — "अहं जगाम" इति वदामि? (*"ahaṃ jagāma" iti vadāmi?*) — pause: the same — "Do I say 'ahaṃ
   jagāma'?"
6. गुरुः — न, "अहमगच्छम्" इति वद। (*na, "aham agaccham" iti vada.*) — pause: न, "अहम् अगच्छम्" इति वद।
   — "No: say 'aham agaccham'."
7. छात्रः — कथायां "राजा जगाम" इति? (*kathāyāṃ "rājā jagāma" iti?*) — pause: the same — "In a story,
   'rājā jagāma'?"
8. गुरुः — आम्, कथायां सम्यक्। (*ām, kathāyāṃ samyak.*) — pause: the same — "Yes, in a story it is
   right." (सम्यक् owned v3.c04.l03.)
Words owned: गुरुः, कः, कुत्र, अहम्, वदति (वद imperative ✓), न, कथा (कथायाम्: loc. of ā-stem ✓), राजा, आम्,
अभ्यासः (V1 "practice"; here the term, a new sense), भवति, अगच्छम् (laṅ 1sg ✓); labels: लिट्, लकारः,
गकारः, जकारः; today's जगाम, उवाच. Seams: उवाचेत्यत्र, को धातुः, वचेति, लिडिति, जगामेत्यत्र, जकारो भवति,
अहमगच्छम्, "…" इति kept apart. None of the exam's items.

**§17 Story — the serial, c03 episode 4: the crocodile went back (perfects; our retelling).**
1. ततो मकरो वानरस्य समीपं पुनर्जगाम। (*tato makaro vānarasya samīpaṃ punar jagāma.*) — pause: ततः
   मकरः वानरस्य समीपम् पुनः जगाम। — "Then the crocodile went back to the monkey."
2. वानरो वृक्षे बभूव। (*vānaro vṛkṣe
   babhūva.*) — pause: वानरः वृक्षे बभूव। — "The monkey was in the tree."
3. मकरस्तमुवाच — "मम पत्नी तव मित्रम्।" (*makaras tam uvāca — "mama patnī tava mitram."*) — pause:
   मकरः तम् उवाच — "मम पत्नी तव मित्रम्।" — "The crocodile said to him: 'My wife is your friend.'"
4. वानरस्तस्मै मधुराणि फलानि ददौ।
   (*vānaras tasmai madhurāṇi phalāni dadau.*) — pause: वानरः तस्मै मधुराणि फलानि ददौ। — "The monkey gave
   him sweet fruits."
5. मकरस्तानि फलानि गृहं निनाय।
   (*makaras tāni phalāni gṛhaṃ nināya.*) — pause: मकरः तानि फलानि गृहम् निनाय। — "The crocodile took
   those fruits home."
निनाय: perfect of नयति
(MW s.v. nī: perf. "nināya"; Pass C), ✓ under the vocabulary-row rule. Words owned: ततः, पुनः, समीपम्,
वृक्षः, तम्, मम, तव, मित्रम्, पत्नी, तस्मै, मधुरः, फलम्, तानि, गृहम्. Seams: ततो मकरो, समीपं पुनर्जगाम
(-उः + voiced → र्), वानरो वृक्षे, मकरस्तमुवाच (-अः + त → स्त; म् + उ), वानरस्तस्मै, मकरस्तानि, गृहं निनाय.

**§18 Exercises.**
- **Ex 1 (obj 1; class 6 min)** — "Write the 3sg, 3du and 3pl perfect": (1) गम् (2) कृ (3) भू (4) दा (5)
  वच् (6) पत् (7) श्रु (8) ब्रू (आह). Key: the production table. Three points per verb.
- **Ex 2 (T10; class 3 min)** — "Write the nine P perfect endings in their table." Key: T10. (Serves
  obj 1.)
- **Ex 3 (obj 2; derive it yourself; homework 12 min)** — "Derive, according to Pāṇini, in table form":
  (1) दा → ददौ (2) पठ् → पपाठ (3) पच् → पपाच (4) पा → पपौ (5) चर् → चचार (6) या → ययौ. Key: E-j, E-k.
- **Ex 4 (obj 3; class 7 min, N1 read in class)** — questions on N1: (1) Where is the city, and what is
  its name? (on the bank of the Bhāgīrathī; Pāṭaliputra) (2) Who was its king? (Sudarśana) (3) What did
  the king hear one day? (a pair of verses being recited) (4) What did he hear one night? (a sound of
  weeping) (5) Name the root and lakāra of शुश्राव and of निर्जगाम (श्रु, liṭ; गम् with निस्, liṭ) (6) What
  did the king take before he went out? (his sword). One point each; (5) one point for both roots.
- **Ex 5 (obj 4; production; homework 8 min)** — "Tell the episode of §17 in your own six sentences,
  with at least four different 3rd-person perfects, joined, with the pause form." Key: a model answer
  (§17's five lines + "मकरो गृहं जगाम।"); scoring: one point per sentence with a correct perfect and
  correct seams; accept any owned perfect of the production table or the vocabulary rows.
- **Ex 6 (§9–§11; homework 2 min)** — "Read E-i aloud, naming the markers."

**§19 Quiz (10; homework).**
1. According to Pāṇini (3.2.115), liṭ is for the past that is: (a) out of sight (परोक्षे) (b) witnessed
   (c) recent (d) habitual — a. TRADITION
2. According to Pāṇini (6.1.8), before liṭ the root is: (a) doubled (b) shortened (c) deleted (d)
   given guṇa — a. TRADITION
3. The ending of जगाम is: (a) णल् (b) तिप् (c) अतुस् (d) उस् — a. TRADITION
4. In जगाम, ज comes from ग by: (a) 7.4.62 कुहोश्चुः (b) 7.4.60 (c) 7.2.116 (d) 6.1.8 — a. TRADITION
5. The 3pl perfect of गम् is: (a) जग्मुः (b) जगाम (c) जग्मतुः (d) गच्छन्ति — a. FACT (Whitney §800;
   Kāśikā under 6.4.98)
6. The 3sg perfect of दा is: (a) ददौ (b) ददाति (c) ददतुः (d) ददुः — a. FACT
7. According to Pāṇini, why is there no vṛddhi in बभूव? (a) 1.2.6 makes liṭ after भू k-marked (b) भू
   has no vowel (c) णल् has no ण् (d) the root is not doubled — a. TRADITION
8. Whitney says of the later language's perfect: (a) it is a past tense, equivalent with the imperfect
   (b) it is used only for what was not witnessed (c) it is never used in prose (d) it is a future —
   a. FACT [REF:whitney1889 §821]
9. According to Pāṇini (3.4.84), आह is: (a) a substitute in the laṭ of ब्रू (b) a perfect of √अह् (c)
   an aorist (d) an imperative — a. TRADITION (b is Whitney's account: true as Whitney's, not
   Pāṇini's)
10. The 3sg perfect of वच् is: (a) उवाच (b) ऊचुः (c) वदति (d) अब्रवीत् — a. FACT

**§20 bookings (verbatim).**
- +1 day: six perfects — write the 3rd singular and the 3rd plural perfect of गम्, कृ, भू, दा, वच् and
  श्रु, from the production table; v4.c03.l05's Station 0 runs exactly this.
- +1 week: derive जगाम — derive, according to Pāṇini, in table form, गम् + लिट् → जगाम, each step with
  its sūtra; v4.c03.l06's Station 0 runs exactly this.

**Class timing (45).** 0–7 Station 0 (S0-ee 3, S0-ed 4) · 7–9 review prompts (three) · 9–11 hook (उवाच,
ददौ, जगाम) · 11–14 3.2.115 with padaccheda; the IR-019 box; the TRADITION/FACT callout (3) · 14–20 E-i with
the teacher, the D rules read from the table (6) · 20–23 T10 and Ex 2 (3) · 23–27 the production table
and the K card, E-l in two columns (4) · 27–33 Ex 1 (6) · 33–35 the आह triplet (2) · 35–42 N1 read
joined, glossed, Ex 4 (7) · 42–45 model sentences aloud (3). Homework: Ex 3, Ex 5, Ex 6, §15, §16, §17,
the quiz.

**§21 Homework (45).** Ex 3 (12) · Ex 5 (8) · Ex 6 (2) · §15 (T10 learned; the K card read) (4) · §16
aloud (4) · §17 read (3) · N1 re-read aloud, joined then pause form (3) · the quiz (7) and key (2). = 45.

**Teacher Notes.** K27–K35, K52 (6.1.17, 1.2.5, 1.2.6) with their corrections; Kāśikā 6.4.98 (lines
65224–65226: *jagmatuḥ, jagmuḥ*), 6.4.64 (64537–64541: *papatuḥ, papuḥ*), 6.4.120 (65656–65661:
*pecatuḥ*); 1.3.7 (the ण् of णल्); 1.1.59 (दौ doubled as दा); 6.1.108 (u + a → u); 6.4.120; 6.4.98; 6.4.64;
7.4.73 भवतेरः and 6.4.88 भुवो वुग्लुङ्लिटोः and 8.4.54 (बभूव's अ, व and ब); 7.1.91 णलुत्तमो वा (जगम beside
जगाम in the 1sg); 6.1.4, 6.1.1, 3.4.115 (liṭ ārdhadhātuka); 6.4.77 (शुश्रुवुः); the N1 printing changes;
the X1–X2 quotations; the Kāśikā's line under 3.2.111 on laṅ for a well-known parokṣa event (K43, l06).

**Main risk (plan):** the perfect reached for in the 1st person — fixed by teaching it as the narrator's
tense, 3rd person; "I went" is अगच्छम्.

## 5. L5 — लृट्: The Future, with the Card of the Other Four Lakāras (grammar)

**Objectives.**
1. en "Form the future, parasmaipada and ātmanepada, of ten owned verbs from the future stems on the
   vocabulary card." · hi "शब्द-कार्ड के भविष्यत्-अङ्गों से दस परिचित क्रियाओं के लृट् रूप (परस्मैपद और
   आत्मनेपद) बनाएँ।" (Ex 1, 10 items)
2. en "Derive, according to Pāṇini, करिष्यति with the teacher, and गमिष्यति, पठिष्यति, भविष्यति,
   नेष्यति, दास्यति and हरिष्यति by yourself." · hi "पाणिनि के अनुसार शिक्षक के साथ करिष्यति की, और स्वयं
   छह रूपों की व्युत्पत्ति करें।" (Ex 3, 6 items)
3. en "Name the lakāra of eight forms from the card of the other four." · hi "बाकी चार लकारों के कार्ड
   से आठ रूपों के लकार पहचानें।" (Ex 2, 8 items)
4. en "Say what each character will do, in six sentences with futures and मा भैषीः, joined, with the
   pause form beneath." · hi "भविष्यत् रूपों और मा भैषीः से छह वाक्यों में बताएँ कि हर पात्र क्या करेगा।"
   (Ex 4, 6 items)

**The opening (§1).** Volume 3 glossed करिष्यामि "I will do" in the Hitopadeśa (v3.c06.l02) and named
गमिष्यति "will go" as Volume 4's (v3.c03.l03). Question: what is the -इष्य- of करिष्यामि, and why is the
ष the ष of रामेषु?

**The one grammar point (plan).**
- The future (लृट्; 3.3.13) adds the sign स्य to the root (3.1.33), then the present endings (T6).
- स्य is ārdhadhātuka (3.4.114), so it takes guṇa (7.3.84) and, after most roots, the augment इट्
  (7.2.35). The स then becomes ष after इ (8.3.59, owned).

**Forms taught (T12; Whitney §§931–939; memorised: T12, plan §1.3.2).**

| Root (owned verb) | Future stem | 3sg · 3du · 3pl | Note (Whitney §935 / Apte's root entry; Pass C) |
|---|---|---|---|
| भू (भवति) | भविष्य- | भविष्यति · भविष्यतः · भविष्यन्ति | seṭ (§932) |
| कृ (करोति) | करिष्य- | करिष्यति (Ā करिष्यते) | iṭ by 7.2.70 ऋद्धनोः स्ये |
| गम् (गच्छति) | गमिष्य- | गमिष्यति | iṭ in P by 7.2.58 गमेरिट् परस्मैपदेषु |
| पठ् (पठति) | पठिष्य- | पठिष्यति | seṭ |
| दा (ददाति) | दास्य- | दास्यति | aniṭ (§932: "दास्य `dāsyá`") |
| नी (नयति) | नेष्य- | नेष्यति | aniṭ |
| दृश् (पश्यति) | द्रक्ष्य- | द्रक्ष्यति | aniṭ (Teacher Notes for -क्ष्-) |
| वच् (उवाच; ब्रवीति's future) | वक्ष्य- | वक्ष्यति | aniṭ (Teacher Notes for -क्ष्-) |
| लभ् (लभते) | लप्स्य- | लप्स्यते | Ā, aniṭ (Teacher Notes for -प्-) |
| स्था (तिष्ठति) | स्थास्य- | स्थास्यति | aniṭ |

**All persons (✓; P and Ā, sg. and pl., 3du — the D13 formula), on T6:**

  (K37).
- **D — 3.4.114 आर्धधातुकं शेषः** — "Every other affix after a root (not a tiṅ, not ś-marked) is called

- **D — 7.2.35 आर्धधातुकस्येड् वलादेः** — "An ārdhadhātuka affix beginning with a वल् sound takes the
  augment इट्." [REF:kashika 7.2.35] (K39). One learner line: "वल् = every consonant except य (from
  the व of वल् to the ल् of हल्): स्य begins with स, a वल् sound" (the pratyāhāra of c01, expanded).
- **D — 7.2.70 ऋद्धनोः स्ये** — "After an ṛ-final root, and after हन्, स्य takes इट्." [REF:kashika
  7.2.70] (K40: *kariṣyati / hariṣyati / haniṣyati*).
- **D — 7.2.58 गमेरिट् परस्मैपदेषु** — "After गम्, an s-initial ārdhadhātuka affix takes इट् in the
  parasmaipada." [REF:kashika 7.2.58] (K41: *gamiṣyati*).

Locus: Pañcatantra 4 (Labdhapraṇāśa), frame story: the prose after Panc 4.5 (`vispancu.htm`, line 5228)
and the wife's reply after Panc 4.6 (line 5235). E-text, IAST, character by character:
> *atha tayābhihitam-yaḥ sarvadaivāmṛta-prāyāṇīdṛśāni phalāni bhakṣayati, tasya hṛdayam amṛta-mayaṃ
> bhaviṣyati | tad yadi bhāryayā te prayojanaṃ, tatas tasya hṛdayaṃ mahyaṃ prayaccha |* … *tan nūnaṃ
> sā vānarī bhaviṣyati, yatas tasyā anurāgataḥ sakalam api dinaṃ tatra gamayasi |*

(The first block continues *yena tad bhakṣayitvā jarā-maraṇa-rahitā tvayā saha bhogān bhunajmi |*, cut
at the daṇḍa; the second begins *atha makary āha-tvayā kadācid api mama vacanaṃ nānyathā kṛtam |*, cut
before *tan*; both cuts marked by the ellipsis.)

Printed joined (changes for Teacher Notes: hyphens dropped; the speech-frame dash spaced):
> अथ तयाभिहितम् — यः सर्वदैवामृतप्रायाणीदृशानि फलानि भक्षयति, तस्य हृदयममृतमयं भविष्यति। तद्यदि
> भार्यया ते प्रयोजनम्, ततस्तस्य हृदयं मह्यं प्रयच्छ। … तन्नूनं सा वानरी भविष्यति, यतस्तस्या अनुरागतः
> सकलमपि दिनं तत्र गमयसि।

**Printing check** (STYLE_GUIDE §3.1, no sandhi before a comma): the e-text has *prayojanaṃ,* with
anusvāra before the comma; the course prints **प्रयोजनम्,** at the comma (the c02-era rule; listed among
the Teacher-Notes changes). Pause form, translation and inventory: plan §5.2 (re-checked: **37 tokens, 7
glossed, about 1 in 5.3**; सर्वदा T, built in v3.c05.l03; वानरी L). Source line: "Pañcatantra 4, frame
story (prose after 4.5 and after 4.6); text of the GRETIL e-text [REF:panchatantra]".

**Model sentences** (the plan's six; checked; seams per row 0.29).
1. श्वोऽहं ग्रामं गमिष्यामि। (*śvo 'haṃ grāmaṃ gamiṣyāmi.*) — pause: श्वः अहम् ग्रामम् गमिष्यामि। —
   "Tomorrow I shall go to the village." — कल मैं गाँव जाऊँगा।
2. यदि त्वं पठिष्यसि, ज्ञानं लप्स्यसे। (*yadi tvaṃ paṭhiṣyasi, jñānaṃ lapsyase.*) — pause: यदि त्वम्
   पठिष्यसि, ज्ञानम् लप्स्यसे। — "If you study, you will gain knowledge." — यदि तुम पढ़ोगे, तो ज्ञान
   पाओगे।
3. नूनं स आगमिष्यति। (*nūnaṃ sa āgamiṣyati.*) — pause: नूनम् सः आगमिष्यति। — "Surely he will come." —
   वह ज़रूर आएगा। (सः before a vowel: स + gap; आगमिष्यति: the future of the owned आगच्छति, rule 17.)
4. मा भैषीः, अहं तव साहाय्यं करिष्यामि। (*mā bhaiṣīḥ, ahaṃ tava sāhāyyaṃ kariṣyāmi.*) — pause: मा
   भैषीः, अहम् तव साहाय्यम् करिष्यामि। — "Do not be afraid; I will help you." — डरो मत, मैं तुम्हारी
   सहायता करूँगा। (V3 B2's line, now taught; साहाय्यम् v3.c06)
5. तस्य हृदयेन मम प्रयोजनम्। (*tasya hṛdayena mama prayojanam.*) — pause: the same — "I need his heart"
   (lit. "with his heart is my purpose"). — मुझे उसके हृदय से काम है।
6. राजा ते वर्तनं दास्यति। (*rājā te vartanaṃ dāsyati.*) — pause: राजा ते वर्तनम् दास्यति। — "The king
   will give you a wage." — राजा तुम्हें वेतन देगा।

| वर्तनम् | vartanam | /ʋɐɾt̪ɐn̪ɐm/ | livelihood; wages | रोज़ी, वेतन | n. a-stem | ledger-new (-; a kṛt noun of वर्तते, lexicalised, rule 18) | MW s.v. vartana n. "livelihood, subsistence, occupation, earnings, wages"; Apte s.v. vartana (Pass C: the n. sense); the Vīravara thread's word (N6, N8: *kiṃ te vartanam?*) |

**§14 line:** "Ledger 558 → 565 (7 ledger-new). Cells drilled: 14 (T12: seven P and seven Ā cells on
करोति and लभते). Tools added: 6 (3.3.13, 3.1.33, 3.4.114, 7.2.35, 7.2.70, 7.2.58 D)."

**§3 Station 0 (verbatim) and review prompts.**
- +1 day: six perfects — write the 3rd singular and the 3rd plural perfect of गम्, कृ, भू, दा, वच् and
  श्रु, from the production table; v4.c03.l05's Station 0 runs exactly this.
- +1 week: the anubandha card — say what each of these markers does and name its rule: the ट् of लट्,
  the ञ् of डुकृञ्, the श् of शप्, the प् of तिप्, the missing प् of तस्, the ण् of णल्; v4.c03.l05's
  Station 0 runs exactly this.
Keys: S0-eg, S0-ef. Review prompts: (v4.c02.l03) "Why ष in रामेषु?" — the स् of the affix after ए (an
इण् sound), not word-final: 8.3.59; (v3.c06.l02) "करिष्यामि — what did Volume 3 call it?" — "I will do", a
glossed future (Volume 4); (v3.c03.l01) "Say पठति in the plural, all persons" — पठन्ति, पठथ, पठामः.

**§4 Historical Context.** **[TRADITION · परम्परा]** the leṭ line (row 0.24) as the callout, with the
Kāśikā's same list under 3.4.77 named in one sentence: "The Kāśikā (c. 7th century CE) lists the same
ten l-affixes in the same order under 3.4.77. [REF:kashika 3.4.77]; [REF:laghukaumudi]"

**§5 Scientific Explanation (FACT).** Whitney §931: "THE verb has two futures, of very different age and
character …"; §932 (the stem, quoted in row 0.22); §948 (use; the conjecture sentence for N2); §826 and
§579 for the card; §949 for luṭ. [REF:whitney1889 §931]; [REF:whitney1889 §932]; [REF:whitney1889 §948];
[REF:whitney1889 §826]; [REF:whitney1889 §579]; [REF:whitney1889 §949]. The DCS lines (row 0.27) only if
the `dcs` key exists. **Hindi parallel (row 0.32 (a)), one FACT callout:** "**[FACT · तथ्य]** Hindi
भविष्य 'the future' is the Sanskrit word भविष्य, whose letters are those of the future stem भविष्य-
(Monier-Williams: 'future, imminent, impending'); Hindi took it whole, with the ष that 8.3.59 made in
Sanskrit after the augment इ. [REF:kachru2006]; [REF:monierwilliams1899 s.v. bhaviṣya]"

**§6 Sanskrit Explanation.** Model sentences 1, 2, and: करिष्यतीत्यत्र स्य इति प्रत्ययः। (*kariṣyatīty
atra sya iti pratyayaḥ.*) — pause: करिष्यति इति अत्र स्य इति प्रत्ययः। — "In kariṣyati the affix is sya."
Joined: करिष्यतीत्यत्र स्येति प्रत्ययः। (*kariṣyatīty atra syeti pratyayaḥ.*) — pause as above (स्य
+ इति → स्येति, अ + इ → ए; स्य a label).

**§7 Hindi Explanation.** Natively written: लृट्: धातु + स्य + लट् के प्रत्यय; इट् आगम (कहाँ आता है, कहाँ
नहीं — शब्द-कार्ड देखें); ष का कारण वही जो रामेषु में; बाकी चार लकार (पहचान के लिए); मा भैषीः. Terms:
लृट्, आर्धधातुक, इट्, लुट्, लृङ्, लुङ्, आशीर्लिङ्.

**§8 English Explanation.** The recipe: (1) करिष्यामि, गमिष्यति; (2) what is -इष्य-? (3) the tools:
3.3.13, 3.1.33, 3.4.114, 7.2.35/70/58, 7.3.84, 8.3.59; (4) E-m; (5) E-n; (6) sentences. The iṭ line
("lexical; the vocabulary card prints each stem"); the homograph line (row 0.15); the card of the other
four (recognise and name, never produce); मा भैषीः; the ten-count line.

**§9–§11.** Row 0.36: E-m read aloud ("लृट् — the ऋ-sign and ट् are markers … what is left is ल्";
"इट् — the ट् is a marker; what you hear is इ"); करिष्यति /kɐɾiʂjɐt̪i/; भविष्यति /bʱɐʋiʂjɐt̪i/; the ष heard
as in रामेषु; मा भैषीः /mɑː bʱɐi̯ʂiːh/ (ऐ /ɐi̯/, STYLE_GUIDE §2.1).

**§12 Common mistakes.**

| # | Mistake | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Guessing iṭ by rule (*दासिष्यति, *नयिष्यति) | the future stem is a vocabulary fact (the card); the rule only explains it |
| 2 | *करस्यति / *करिस्यति | इट् by 7.2.70; then स् after इ → ष् (8.3.59) |
| 3 | Taking the -इ- of पठिष्यति for the 1sg Ā ending इट् | it is the augment इट् (7.2.35) — the same augment as in स्वपिति (7.2.76, l02); लप्स्ये ends in the ending इट् (→ ए), with no augment |
| 4 | Producing a ◐ form (अभूत्, भविता) in your own sentences | the four on the card are recognised and named only; मा भैषीः is the one chunk |
| 5 | मा + future or *मा भवसि | the prohibitive you own is मा + imperative (मा वद); मा भैषीः is a chunk |
| 6 | Reading भविष्यति in N2 only as "will be" | it can state a supposition, "must be" (Whitney §948) |

**§13 Examples.** E-m; T12; the card (table 0.D) with √भू and √कृ; N2 with its glosses; the leṭ line.

**§15 Grammar.** T12 (memorised); the future-stem card; the card of the other four (◐); मा भैषीः; E-m.
**Toolbox (cumulative 89 = 83 + 6).** Paradigm ledger: ✓ the future, P and Ā, of every owned verb whose
stem the card prints; ✓ मा भैषीः; ◐ luṭ, lṛṅ, luṅ, āśīrliṅ; ○ leṭ.

**Previewed / Deferred (plan).** Previewed: the future participle करिष्यन् (c04.l01 card). Deferred: the
periphrastic future, conditional, aorist and benedictive, all ◐ (Volume 5 reading); मा + aorist beyond
मा भैषीः, ◐ (Volume 5 reading; its production is not planned, §6; review volume:B#0). **Payoff
(plan):** करिष्यामि, the one glossed future of Volume 3, is made by rule; the 1.x गच्छ "future" row
(गमिष्यति) is restored; 8.3.59 works again inside a new form.

**§16 Conversation (10 turns; homework).**
1. छात्रः — गुरो, करिष्यतीत्यत्र कः प्रत्ययः? (*guro, kariṣyatīty atra kaḥ pratyayaḥ?*) — pause: गुरो,
   करिष्यति इति अत्र कः प्रत्ययः? — "Teacher, in kariṣyati, what is the affix?"
2. गुरुः — स्येति प्रत्ययः, इडागमश्च। (*syeti pratyayaḥ, iḍāgamaś ca.*) — pause: स्य इति प्रत्ययः,
   इट्-आगमः च। — "The affix is sya, and the augment iṭ."
3. छात्रः — दास्यतीत्यत्र इकारः कुत्र? (*dāsyatīty atra ikāraḥ kutra?*) — joined: दास्यतीत्यत्रेकारः
   (*dāsyatīty atrekāraḥ*: अ + इ → ए) — pause: दास्यति इति अत्र इकारः कुत्र? — "In dāsyati, where is the
   i?"
4. गुरुः — देत्यत्र नास्ति। (*detyatra nāsti.*) — pause: दा इति अत्र न अस्ति। — "In dā there is none."
5. छात्रः — श्वः किं करिष्यसि? (*śvaḥ kiṃ kariṣyasi?*) — pause: श्वः किम् करिष्यसि? — "What will you do
   tomorrow?"
6. गुरुः — श्वो ग्रामं गमिष्यामि। (*śvo grāmaṃ gamiṣyāmi.*) — pause: श्वः ग्रामम् गमिष्यामि। — "Tomorrow
   I shall go to the village."
7. छात्रः — अहं गृहे पठिष्यामि। (*ahaṃ gṛhe paṭhiṣyāmi.*) — pause: अहम् गृहे पठिष्यामि। — "I shall
   study at home."
8. गुरुः — यदि पठिष्यसि, सर्वं ज्ञास्यसि। (*yadi paṭhiṣyasi, sarvaṃ jñāsyasi.*) — pause: यदि पठिष्यसि,
   सर्वम् ज्ञास्यसि। — "If you study, you will know everything."
9. छात्रः — श्वो भयं मे भविष्यति। (*śvo bhayaṃ me bhaviṣyati.*) — pause: श्वः भयम् मे भविष्यति। — "Tomorrow I shall
   be afraid." (परीक्षा "exam" is not in the ledger: not used.)
10. गुरुः — मा भैषीः। (*mā bhaiṣīḥ.*) — "Do not be afraid."
Words owned: गुरुः, कः, किम्, कुत्र, श्वः, ग्रामः, अहम्, गृहम्, सर्वम्, मे, न अस्ति; labels: प्रत्ययः (c02),
आगमः (c02), इकारः, स्य, इट्; today's यदि, भयम् (l01), मा भैषीः, the futures. Seams: करिष्यतीत्यत्र, स्येति,
इडागमश्च (ट् → ड् before आ; ः + च → श्च), दास्यतीत्यत्रेकारः, देत्यत्र (आ + इ → ए; इ + अ → य), नास्ति, श्वो
ग्रामं, अहं गृहे, सर्वं ज्ञास्यसि, श्वो भयं. None of the exam's items.

**§17 Story — the serial, c03 episode 5: the wife's threat and the crocodile's resolve (futures; our
retelling).**
1. भार्या वदति — "यदि तस्य हृदयं न दास्यसि, अहं न जीविष्यामि।" (*bhāryā vadati — "yadi tasya hṛdayaṃ
   na dāsyasi, ahaṃ na jīviṣyāmi."*) — pause: भार्या वदति — "यदि तस्य हृदयम् न दास्यसि, अहम् न
   जीविष्यामि।" — "The wife says: 'If you do not give me his heart, I shall not live.'"
2. मकरश्चिन्तयति — "किं करिष्यामि? वानरो मम सुहृत्, एषा तु मम भार्या।" (*makaraś cintayati — "kiṃ
   kariṣyāmi? vānaro mama suhṛt, eṣā tu mama bhāryā."*) — pause: मकरः चिन्तयति — "किम् करिष्यामि?
   वानरः मम सुहृत्, एषा तु मम भार्या।" — "The crocodile thinks: 'What shall I do? The monkey is my friend,
   but this is my wife.'"
3. स वदति — "मा भैषीः। अहं वानरं गृहं नेष्यामि।" (*sa vadati — "mā bhaiṣīḥ. ahaṃ vānaraṃ gṛhaṃ
   neṣyāmi."*) — pause: सः वदति — "मा भैषीः। अहम् वानरम् गृहम् नेष्यामि।" — "He says: 'Do not be afraid.
   I shall bring the monkey home.'"
4. "तस्य हृदयं तुभ्यं दास्यामि।" (*"tasya hṛdayaṃ tubhyaṃ dāsyāmi."*) — pause: "तस्य हृदयम् तुभ्यम्
   दास्यामि।" — "'I shall give you his heart.'"
5. भार्या वदति — "तदाहं सुखेन जीविष्यामि।" (*bhāryā vadati — "tadāhaṃ sukhena jīviṣyāmi."*) — pause:
   भार्या वदति — "तदा अहम् सुखेन जीविष्यामि।" — "The wife says: 'Then I shall live happily.'"
6. मकरो वानरस्य समीपं गमिष्यति। (*makaro vānarasya samīpaṃ gamiṣyati.*) — pause: मकरः वानरस्य समीपम्
   गमिष्यति। — "The crocodile will go to the monkey."
Words owned: भार्या, हृदयम्, यदि (today), न, अहम्, जीवति (जीविष्यामि: future, the card), मकरः, चिन्तयति,
किम्, वानरः, मम, सुहृत्, एषा, तु, सः, वदति, गृहम्, नयति (नेष्यामि), तुभ्यम्, ददाति (दास्यामि), तदा (v3.c05),
सुखम् (सुख V1; सुखेन ✓), समीपम्, गच्छति (गमिष्यति). Seams: हृदयं न, अहं न, मकरश्चिन्तयति, वानरो मम, स वदति,
अहं वानरं गृहं, हृदयं तुभ्यं, तदाहं (आ + अ → आ), मकरो वानरस्य, समीपं गमिष्यति.

**§18 Exercises.**
- **Ex 1 (obj 1; class 6 min)** — "From the future-stem card, write the form": (1) भू 3pl (2) कृ 1sg (3)
  गम् 2sg (4) पठ् 1pl (5) दा 3du (6) नी 3sg (7) दृश् 3pl (8) लभ् 3sg Ā (9) लभ् 1sg Ā (10) स्था 2pl. Key:
  (1) भविष्यन्ति (2) करिष्यामि (3) गमिष्यसि (4) पठिष्यामः (5) दास्यतः (6) नेष्यति (7) द्रक्ष्यन्ति (8)
  लप्स्यते (9) लप्स्ये (10) स्थास्यथ. One point each.
- **Ex 2 (obj 3; class 4 min)** — "Name the lakāra (the card): (1) भविता (2) अभविष्यत् (3) अभूत् (4)
  भूयात् (5) अगमत् (6) भवितारः (7) अकार्षीत् (8) अभविष्यन्." Key: (1) लुट् (2) लृङ् (3) लुङ् (4) आशीर्लिङ्
  (5) लुङ् (6) लुट् (7) लुङ् (8) लृङ्. Recognition only; never asked to produce.
- **Ex 3 (obj 2; derive it yourself; homework 12 min)** — "Derive, according to Pāṇini, in table form":
  (1) गम् → गमिष्यति (2) पठ् → पठिष्यति (3) भू → भविष्यति (4) नी → नेष्यति (5) दा → दास्यति (6) हृ →
  हरिष्यति. Key: E-n (1)–(6).
- **Ex 4 (obj 4; production; homework 8 min)** — "Say what each character of the serial will do, six
  sentences, joined, with the pause form; one of them with मा भैषीः." Key: §17 lines as a model; scoring
  one point per sentence with a correct future (or the chunk) and correct seams.
- **Ex 5 (N2; class 6 min)** — questions: (1) Who speaks first, and to whom? (the crocodile's wife, to
  him) (2) What does she say the monkey's heart must be? (made of nectar) (3) Why? (he always eats such
  nectar-like fruits) (4) What does she ask for? (his heart) (5) What does she suppose at the end? (that
  it must be a she-monkey) (6) Find the two futures and say their root (भविष्यति ×2: भू). (Serves obj 1
  and the reading; 6 items.)
- **Ex 6 (§9–§11; homework 2 min)** — "Read the करिष्यति table aloud, naming the markers."

**§19 Quiz (10; homework).**
1. According to Pāṇini (3.1.33), the sign of lṛṭ is: (a) स्य (b) शप् (c) तास् (d) णल् — a. TRADITION
2. According to Pāṇini (3.4.114), स्य is: (a) ārdhadhātuka (b) sārvadhātuka (c) a tiṅ (d) a marker — a.
   TRADITION
3. In करिष्यति, the इ is: (a) the augment इट् (7.2.70) (b) the ending इट् (c) part of the root (d) a
   class sign — a. TRADITION
4. The ष of करिष्यति comes from स by the rule that also makes: (a) रामेषु (b) रामेण (c) नयति (d) जगाम —
   a. TRADITION
5. The 3pl future of भू is: (a) भविष्यन्ति (b) भवन्ति (c) बभूवुः (d) अभवन् — a. FACT [REF:whitney1889 §932]
6. The 3sg future of दा is: (a) दास्यति (b) ददाति (c) ददौ (d) देहि — a. FACT
7. मा भैषीः means: (a) do not be afraid (b) you will be afraid (c) I am not afraid (d) fear is — a.
   (the chunk)
8. अभूत् is: (a) an aorist (लुङ्), recognised only (b) a future (c) a perfect (d) an imperative — a.
   TRADITION (the card)
9. According to the Laghusiddhāntakaumudī, which of the ten l-affixes belongs to the Veda only? (a)
   लेट् (b) लोट् (c) लिट् (d) लृङ् — a. TRADITION
10. Whitney says that in the later language the periphrastic future's sense of "a definite time to
    come" is: (a) faintly traceable (b) always strict (c) the commonest use (d) unknown — a. FACT
    [REF:whitney1889 §949]

**§20 bookings (verbatim).**
- +1 day: eight futures — say and write the 3rd singular and the 3rd plural future of करोति, गच्छति,
  पठति, ददाति, नयति, पश्यति, लभते and तिष्ठति, from the future stems on your vocabulary card; v4.c03.l06's
  Station 0 runs exactly this.
- +1 week: derive करिष्यति — derive, according to Pāṇini, in table form, कृ + लृट् → करिष्यति, each step
  with its sūtra; v4.c04.l01's Station 0 runs exactly this.

the board (2) · 19–25 Ex 1 (6) · 25–28 the iṭ line and the homograph line (3) · 28–32 the card of the
other four, मा भैषीः, the ten-count line (4) · 32–36 Ex 2 (4) · 36–42 N2 read joined and glossed, Ex 5
(6) · 42–45 model sentences aloud (3). Homework: Ex 3, Ex 4, Ex 6, §15, §16, §17, the quiz.

its pass mark (5), the real-world task (3)) · prerequisites [v4.c03.l05] · review_of [v4.c03.l01,
