`v4c03l06-bhu-table.svg` (the ten rows, six solid ✓, four outlined ◐, leṭ greyed ○); worksheet
`v4c03l06-exam-booklet.pdf`; quizzes `v4c03l06-quiz.md`, `v4c03l06-quiz-key.md`. Ledger stays 565.

**Objectives.**
1. en "Fill the eighteen 3rd-person cells of the six productive rows of the √भू table, name the other
   four rows, and read one unseen rule about a lakāra." · hi "भू-तालिका की छह उत्पादक पंक्तियों के अठारह
   प्रथम-पुरुष रूप भरें, बाकी चार पंक्तियों के नाम बताएँ, और लकार का एक अनदेखा सूत्र पढ़ें।" (task A)
2. en "Name the class, class sign and present stem of twelve owned verbs." · hi "बारह परिचित क्रियाओं का
   गण, विकरण और वर्तमान-अङ्ग बताएँ।" (task B, 12 items)
3. en "Fill eight strong and weak cells and say why, according to Pāṇini." · hi "आठ सबल-निर्बल रूप भरें
   और पाणिनि के अनुसार कारण बताएँ।" (task C, 8 items)
4. en "Read an unseen Hitopadeśa dialogue with its glosses and answer six questions." · hi "हितोपदेश का
   एक अनदेखा संवाद पढ़ें और छह प्रश्नों के उत्तर दें।" (task D, 6 items)
5. en "Tell an episode of the serial with four perfects, then say what each character will do with four
   futures." · hi "धारावाहिक का एक प्रसंग चार लिट् रूपों से कहें, फिर चार लृट् रूपों से बताएँ कि हर पात्र
   क्या करेगा।" (task E, 8 sentences)

**§3 Station 0 (verbatim; before the sitting, outside its 45 minutes).**
- +1 day: eight futures — say and write the 3rd singular and the 3rd plural future of करोति, गच्छति,
  पठति, ददाति, नयति, पश्यति, लभते and तिष्ठति, from the future stems on your vocabulary card; v4.c03.l06's
  Station 0 runs exactly this.
- +1 week: derive जगाम — derive, according to Pāṇini, in table form, गम् + लिट् → जगाम, each step with
  its sūtra; v4.c03.l06's Station 0 runs exactly this.
Keys: S0-ei, S0-eh. One review prompt reaches v3.c03.l05: "Name the four present-system lakāras" — लट्,
लङ्, लोट्, विधिलिङ्. **Exam guard:** row 0.37 (checked item by item below: S0-ei's eight verbs give no
cell of task A, whose cells are all of भू; task B's twelve verbs ask class, sign and stem, not futures;
no task asks a perfect derivation; task E is free production, and its key does not require गम्).

**§4 Historical Context.** One line, no callout: "The table on this page is the classical one, ten rows;
the tradition's own ten l-affixes count liṅ once and include the Vedic leṭ (lesson 5)."

**§5–§11.** Brief, as in the Chapter 2 exam: §5 the FACT lines the chapter used (Whitney §§553, 604, 607,
631–637, 714, 734, 793, 800, 801, 821, 826, 932, 940, 944, 948–949), one sentence each; §6 the model
sentences of l01–l05 that are not exam items (l01 1, 3, 4; l02 2, 4, 6; l03 1, 6; l04 1, 3; l05 1, 3) read
aloud; §7 Hindi: how the exam works and its pass rule, natively written; §8 English: the assembly and the
pass rule; §9–§11: the readings of §6 joined then pause form; the spoken half of task E.

**§12 Common mistakes (the chapter's, as exam traps).**

| # | Mistake | Fix |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Producing a ◐ row's cells (भविता, अभूत्) as if asked | name the lakāra and its use; the ◐ cells are printed |
| 2 | "laṅ = recent past / past habitual" | 3.2.111: the past not of today |
| 3 | Calling गच्छति, पृच्छति or जानाति "irregular" | 7.3.77; class 6 (श, ṅit: 6.1.16); class 9 with 7.3.79 |
| 4 | A weak cell with guṇa (*करोतः) | तस् has no p: ṅit (1.2.4) → no guṇa (1.1.5) |
| 5 | Taking आह for a perfect "according to Pāṇini" | according to Pāṇini it stands for ब्रू's laṭ (3.4.84) |
| 6 | The perfect in the 1st person in task E | narrate in the 3rd person; "I went" is अगच्छम् |

**§13 Examples (the same types of item as the exam; none of the exam's own; checked against tasks A–E
and the retake sets).** A-type: the √कृ cells of Whitney's synopsis named by lakāra (कर्ता → लुट्;
अकार्षीत् → लुङ्; करिष्यति → लृट्) and one cold-read model: 3.2.115 (taught, Q) read again with its bracket
line. B-type: नयति → 1 · शप् · नय; शृणोति → 5 (by its sign) · श्नु · शृणु-/शृणो-. C-type: एति → strong
(तिप् pit); यन्ति → weak (झि apit). D-type: N1 (b) re-read with three questions. E-type: §17 of l04 and of
l05 as models.

**§14.** "Ledger 565 → 565 (0 ledger-new). Cells drilled: 0 new. Tools added: 0 (toolbox 89)."

**§15 Assembly (plan).** (1) **the √भू table** (table 0.B; the 1.x six-lakāra table of pp. 127–128
restored and completed to ten rows; its laṅ label corrected; the TRADITION line on the ten l-affixes);
(2) the anubandha card (c03.l03); (3) **the toolbox card, 89 tools** (Chapters 1–2's 43 + Chapter 3's
46: 15 + 10 + 7 + 8 + 6), in sūtra-number order, each with its text and one English line — **printed on
a separate sheet, handed out at the start of task C and collected after it** (the D-tier 6.4.110, 6.4.111 are never asked by number without
the card; plan §1.4.1; §7.6 row "v4.c02 fact base", exam shapes) (review c03:E#21); (4) the class card
(table 0.C), T10 and T12, the card of the other four.

**§16 Conversation (8 turns; revision before the sitting; homework).**
1. छात्रा — गुरो, भवतीति लट्, अभवदिति लङ्। (*guro, bhavatīti laṭ, abhavad iti laṅ.*) — pause: गुरो, भवति
   इति लट्, अभवत् इति लङ्। — "Teacher: bhavati is laṭ, abhavat laṅ."
2. गुरुः — आम्। बभूवेति लिट्, भविष्यतीति लृट्। (*ām. babhūveti liṭ, bhaviṣyatīti lṛṭ.*) — pause: आम्।
   बभूव इति लिट्, भविष्यति इति लृट्। — "Yes. babhūva is liṭ, bhaviṣyati lṛṭ."
3. छात्रा — अभूदिति? (*abhūd iti?*) — pause: अभूत् इति? — "And abhūt?"
4. गुरुः — लुङ्, तं मा लिख। (*luṅ, taṃ mā likha.*) — pause: लुङ्, तम् मा लिख। — "luṅ: do not write it
   (yourself)."
5. छात्रा — शृण्वन्तीत्यत्र गुणो नास्ति। (*śṛṇvantīty atra guṇo nāsti.*) — pause: शृण्वन्ति इति अत्र गुणः
   न अस्ति। — "In śṛṇvanti there is no guṇa."
6. गुरुः — आम्, झौ पकारो नास्ति। (*ām, jhau pakāro nāsti.*) — pause: आम्, झौ पकारः न अस्ति। — "Yes: jhi
   has no p."
7. छात्रा — श्वः किं भविष्यति? (*śvaḥ kiṃ bhaviṣyati?*) — pause: श्वः किम् भविष्यति? — "What will happen tomorrow?"
8. गुरुः — मा भैषीः। सर्वं ज्ञास्यसि। (*mā bhaiṣīḥ. sarvaṃ jñāsyasi.*) — pause: मा भैषीः। सर्वम्
   ज्ञास्यसि। — "Do not be afraid. You will know it all."
Words owned: गुरुः, आम्, सः (तम्), मा, लिखति (लिख ✓), श्वः, किम्, सर्वम्, गुणः, न अस्ति; labels: the lakāra
names, पकारः, झि; the chapter's forms. Seams: भवतीति, अभवदिति (त् → द् before a vowel), बभूवेति (अ + इ →
ए), भविष्यतीति, अभूदिति, तं मा, शृण्वन्तीत्यत्र, गुणो नास्ति, पकारो नास्ति, सर्वं ज्ञास्यसि. None of the exam's
items.

**§17 The spiral (unscored; after the sitting; plan §4.0.5).**
(a) **The Chapter 2 item, रामेण, derived according to Pāṇini** (c02 D-j, four columns; now with the
toolbox of 89 open): 0 राम + टा · 1 राम + इन — 7.1.12 (D) · 2 रामेन — 6.1.87 (owned) · 3 र … न — 8.4.1
(Q) · 4 रामेण — 8.4.2 (D) · 5 **रामेण**. One line: "The steps are those of Chapter 2; nothing in Chapter 3
changes them."
(b) **The thread verse re-read** (Hit 0.36, `hitop_u.htm` lines 233–234; the source label as v3.c02.l05
prints it, [REF:hitopadesha prastāvikā 36]):
> उद्यमेन हि सिध्यन्ति कार्याणि न मनोरथैः। न हि सुप्तस्य सिंहस्य प्रविशन्ति मुखे मृगाः॥
> (*udyamena hi sidhyanti kāryāṇi na manorathaiḥ | na hi suptasya siṃhasya praviśanti mukhe mṛgāḥ ||*)
> (pause form: the same; no seam is joined in the printed line)
The learner names what Chapter 3 has added: (1) **सिध्यन्ति** — a class-4 present: सिध् + श्यन् (3.1.69,
K) → सिध्य-; no guṇa, because श्यन् has no प्: ṅit (1.2.4, 1.1.5); झि → -अन्ति (7.1.3); (2) **प्रविशन्ति**
— a class-6 present (विश् + श, 3.1.77, K; no guṇa, the same reason). Key printed after the sitting. Not
asked: सुप्तस्य (a kta participle, c04.l01). The e-text writes *nahi*; the course prints न हि, as v3.c02.l05
did (Teacher Notes).

**§18 The exam (one 45-minute sitting; closed book except the toolbox card for task C; the booklet
prints every task; 2 minutes of instructions inside the 45 — so the tasks are A 11, B 7, C 7, D 10, E 8
= 43 + 2; plan: A 8 + 4, B 8, C 7, D 10, E 8 with no instruction time, §8 deviation 11).**

**Task A — the √भू table and a rule read cold (11 min; 26 points; pass 19).**
(A1, 7 min) "Fill the 3rd singular, dual and plural of भू in the six rows that are blank: laṭ, laṅ,
loṭ, vidhiliṅ, liṭ, lṛṭ. For each of the four rows printed filled, write its lakāra name and its use in
a few words." The booklet prints the ten rows, the four ◐ rows filled (भविता भवितारौ भवितारः · अभविष्यत्
अभविष्यताम् अभविष्यन् · अभूत् अभूताम् अभूवन् · भूयात् भूयास्ताम् भूयासुः), in this shuffled order: लृट्
(blank), the luṅ row, लट् (blank), the luṭ row, लिट् (blank), लङ् (blank), the āśīrliṅ row, लोट् (blank),
विधिलिङ् (blank), the lṛṅ row; the blank rows carry their lakāra names, the filled rows carry none. Key:
table 0.B. Scoring: 1 per ✓ cell (18); 1 per ◐ row with name and use right (4: लुङ् — a past (aorist) ·
लुट् — the periphrastic future, a future not of today · आशीर्लिङ् — a blessing, "may …" · लृङ् — the
conditional, "would have"). **No credit for producing a ◐ cell** (plan main risk).
(A2, 4 min) "Read this rule in the case code. (1) Which word is the affix, which gives its sense? (2)
What does the heading in the bracket add? (3) So what is laṅ for, according to Pāṇini? (4) Correct the
two labels the 1.x book gave laṅ: 'recent past' and 'past habitual'." Printed: **3.2.111 अनद्यतने लङ्**
with its bracket line (table 0.H). Key: (1) लङ् — प्रथमा, the affix; अनद्यतने — सप्तमी, the sense "not of
today"; (2) भूते: "in the past" (with [after a root] from धातोः); (3) "after a root, laṅ for the past not
of today" (Kāśikā, Teacher Notes: *avidyamānādyatane bhūte 'rthe*); (4) neither: the past not of today,
whether recent or not, habitual or not. 1 point each. Remediation: l05 §15 and the √भू table (A1); l01
§15 (the case code of 3.2.123, 3.2.115) and the c01 case code (A2).

**Task B — class detective (7 min; 36 points; pass 27).** "For each verb write the class (by its first
root), the class sign and the present stem." (1) गच्छति (2) पृच्छति (3) जानाति (4) नृत्यति (5) आप्नोति (6)
करोति (7) गृह्णाति (8) कथयति (9) याति (10) ददाति (11) युनक्ति (12) वदति. Key: (1) 1 भ्वादिः · शप् · गच्छ
(7.3.77) — trap (2) 6 तुदादिः · श · पृच्छ (no guṇa; 6.1.16) — trap (3) 9 क्र्यादिः · श्ना · जाना-/जानी- (ज्ञा
→ जा, 7.3.79) — trap (4) 4 दिवादिः · श्यन् · नृत्य (5) 5 स्वादिः · श्नु · आप्नु-/आप्नो- (6) 8 तनादिः · उ ·
कुरु-/करो- (either grade scores) (7) 9 · श्ना · गृह्णा-/गृह्णी- (8) 10 चुरादिः · णिच् (with शप्) ·
कथय (9) 2 अदादिः · none (शप् deleted) · या (10) 3 जुहोत्यादिः · none (श्लु, the root doubled) · ददा-/दद्-
(11) 7 रुधादिः · श्नम् · युनज्-/युञ्ज्- (12) 1 · शप् · वद. 1 point each for class, sign, stem; either grade
of a stem scores. The traps are named in the key only. Remediation: l02 §15 (the class card, the
substitute-stem card) and Ex 1, Ex 2.

**Task C — strong and weak (7 min; 16 points; pass 11; the toolbox card open).** "Write the form, and
say, according to Pāṇini, why its stem is strong or weak." (1) श्रु, 3du laṭ (2) कृ, 3du laṭ (3) कृ, 2sg
laṭ (4) अस्, 3du laṭ (5) अस्, 1pl laṭ (6) ब्रू, 3sg laṭ (7) ब्रू, 3du laṭ (8) श्रु, 1pl laṭ. Key: (1) शृणुतः
— weak: तस् is apit, ṅit by 1.2.4, no guṇa by 1.1.5 (2) कुरुतः — weak: the same, and 6.4.110 (अ → उ) (3)
करोषि — strong: सिप् is pit, guṇa (7.3.84) (4) स्तः — weak: ṅit; the अ of अस् dropped (6.4.111) (5) स्मः —
weak: the same (6) ब्रवीति — strong: तिप् is pit, guṇa (ब्रो → ब्रव्; the ई: accept "a rule in Teacher
Notes" or none) (7) ब्रूतः — weak: ṅit, no guṇa (8) शृणुमः (or शृण्मः) — weak: मस् is apit. 1 point for the
form, 1 for the reason (pit/apit with 1.2.4 + 1.1.5 suffices; the D rule earns no extra). Remediation:
l03 §15 and Ex 1–Ex 2.

**Task D — read an unseen passage (10 min; 12 points; pass 8).** Text N8 (plan §5.7; re-found at
`hitop_u.htm` lines 2932–2940; review c03:E#16, P#6). E-text, IAST:
> *śūdraka uvāca-kiṃ te vartanam ? / vīravaro brūte-pratyahaṃ suvarṇa-pañca-śatāni dehi | /
> rājāha-kā te sāmagrī ? / vīravaro brūte-dvau bāhū | tṛtīyaś ca khaḍgaḥ | / rājāha-naitac chakyam |*
Printed joined (hyphens dropped; speech-frame dashes spaced):
> शूद्रक उवाच — किं ते वर्तनम्? वीरवरो ब्रूते — प्रत्यहं सुवर्णपञ्चशतानि देहि। राजाह — का ते सामग्री?
> वीरवरो ब्रूते — द्वौ बाहू। तृतीयश्च खड्गः। राजाह — नैतच्छक्यम्।
Glosses in the margin (4): प्रत्यहम् "every day" · सुवर्णपञ्चशतानि "five hundred gold pieces" · सामग्री
"equipment" · शक्यम् "possible". Named in the margin, never scored: ब्रूते (the Ā present of ब्रवीति,
◐). Names: शूद्रकः, वीरवरः. Gloss ratio: 4 of 24, 1 in 6 (plan §5.7, re-counted from the pause form;
तृतीयः coded L, as the plan keeps it after review c03:E#4; बाहू: बाहु owned V1, the dual ✓). Questions
(2 points each; the passage's own words or English): (1) What does the king first ask Vīravara? — what
his wage is (2) What does Vīravara ask for, and how often? — five hundred gold pieces, every day (3) What
equipment does he name? — his two arms, and a sword as the third (4) What does the king say at the end?
— that it is not possible (5) Name the lakāra of उवाच and of देहि. — लिट् (perfect); लोट् (imperative) (6)
According to Pāṇini, which lakāra does आह stand for? — लट्: it replaces ब्रू's laṭ endings (3.4.84);
accept "laṭ, according to Pāṇini (3.4.84)" (plan §5.7; review c03:E#20; an answer "liṭ" scores 0 here,
because the question is put "according to Pāṇini"). Remediation: l04 (N1, the आह triplet) and l03 (देहि).

**Task E — production (8 min; 16 points; pass 11).** (E1, 6 min, written) "Tell, in four sentences, how
the crocodile went back to the monkey, using four different 3rd-person perfects; then, in four more
sentences, say what the crocodile, his wife and the monkey will do, using four futures. Joined, with the
pause form beneath." (E2, 2 min) Read two of your sentences aloud to the teacher. Key (model, not
required): मकरो वानरस्य समीपं जगाम। वानरो वृक्षे बभूव। मकरस्तमुवाच — "मम भार्या तव मित्रम्।" वानरस्तस्मै
फलानि ददौ। · मकरो वानरं गृहं नेष्यति। भार्या तस्य हृदयं भक्षयिष्यति। वानरः किं करिष्यति? मकरः पुनस्तीरं
गमिष्यति। (pause forms as §17 of l04–l05). Scoring: per sentence 2 points — the verb form right and in the
asked lakāra (1); the sentence correct and joined with a matching pause form (1); E2 is checked, not
scored separately (the spoken half of the plan's "written, then spoken"). Remediation: l04 Ex 5, l05 Ex 4.

**Pass:** every task at its pass mark (A 19/26, B 27/36, C 11/16, D 8/12, E 11/16), and the §19 quiz,
taken after the sitting, at 7/10 (plan §7.6 row "v4.c01 fact base"). Total 106; the rubric weights the
table and the reading (A, D: 38) and the rules (B, C: 52) over free production (E: 16), as the plan's
minutes do.

**Retake (after one week; the alternative item sets, written out in full, Teacher Notes).**
- **A′:** A1 as A (the table is the memorised one; the shuffle changes: लोट्, the lṛṅ row, लिट्, लृट्, the
  āśīrliṅ row, लङ्, the luṭ row, विधिलिङ्, लट्, the luṅ row). A2′: **3.2.110 लुङ्** read cold with its
  bracket line (table 0.H): (1) which word is the affix? (लुङ्, प्रथमा) (2) what does the heading add?
  (भूते: in the past) (3) so what is luṅ for, according to Pāṇini? (the past, with no restriction of
  "today"; Kāśikā K47: *bhūte 'rthe … luṅ*; *akārṣīt*) (4) which row of the table is luṅ, and is it ✓ or
  ◐? (the aorist; ◐).
- **B′:** (1) तिष्ठति — 1 · शप् · तिष्ठ (7.3.78) (2) क्षिपति — 6 · श · क्षिप (3) जायते — 4 · श्यन् · जाय
  (7.3.79) (4) मन्यते — 4 · श्यन् · मन्य (5) शृणोति — 5 (by its sign, 3.1.74) · श्नु · शृणु-/शृणो- (6) अस्ति —
  2 · none · अस्-/स्- (7) स्वपिति — 2 · none · स्वप् (+ इट्) (8) चिन्तयति — 10 · णिच् + शप् · चिन्तय (9)
  मुञ्चति — 6 · श · मुञ्च (10) पिबति — 1 · शप् · पिब (7.3.78) (11) हन्ति — 2 · none · हन्-/हत्-/घ्न्- (12)
  प्राप्नोति — 5 · श्नु · प्राप्नु-/प्राप्नो-. Scoring as B.
- **C′:** (1) श्रु 3pl → शृण्वन्ति (weak) (2) कृ 3pl → कुर्वन्ति (weak; 6.4.110) (3) कृ 1sg → करोमि (strong) (4)
  अस् 3pl → सन्ति (weak; 6.4.111) (5) अस् 2sg → असि (strong) (6) ब्रू 3pl → ब्रुवन्ति (weak) (7) इ 3sg → एति
  (strong) (8) इ 3du → इतः (weak). Scoring as C.
- **D′:** the same passage N8 (it is no longer unseen: §8 open item 5), with six new questions: (1) Who
  speaks first? (Śūdraka, the king) (2) What is Vīravara's wage to be paid in? (gold pieces) (3) How many
  arms does he name? (two) (4) What is his "third"? (a sword) (5) Name the lakāra of ब्रूते, as a form.
  (लट्, a present; ātmanepada: named, not built) (6) Find the word that means "not possible" and say what
  नैतत् is made of. (नैतच्छक्यम्; न + एतत्, अ + ए → ऐ). 2 points each.
- **E′:** "Tell, in four sentences with four perfects, how the monkey gave the crocodile fruits; then, in
  four with futures, what the monkey will do." Scoring as E.

**§19 Quiz (10; after the sitting; 7/10 to pass; none of the exam's items).**
1. According to Pāṇini, the ten classes are named by: (a) the first root of each list (b) their sign (c)
   their meaning (d) their number of roots — a. TRADITION
2. Which class sign has no प्, so that the root before it takes no guṇa? (a) श (b) शप् (c) both (d)
   neither — a. TRADITION
3. The 3sg perfect of कृ is: (a) चकार (b) करोति (c) करिष्यति (d) अकरोत् — a. FACT
4. The 3sg future of स्था is: (a) स्थास्यति (b) तिष्ठति (c) तस्थौ (d) अतिष्ठत् — a. FACT (तस्थौ is printed in
   the vocabulary rows, l04: ✓ as a form)
5. According to Pāṇini, लङ् is for: (a) the past not of today (b) the recent past (c) the habitual past
   (d) the future — a. TRADITION
6. Which is ◐ in this volume? (a) अभविष्यत् (b) भविष्यति (c) बभूव (d) अभवत् — a.
7. According to Pāṇini, before तस् the stem of शृणोति is weak because: (a) तस् is apit, so ṅit (1.2.4) (b)
   तस् is a taddhita (c) तस् has a प् (d) तस् is ārdhadhātuka — a. TRADITION
8. The Kāśikā glosses the domain of liṭ as: (a) the past, not of today, out of sight (b) the present
   (c) a command (d) a wish — a. TRADITION
9. Whitney says the aorist is found in the Hitopadeśa: (a) eight times (b) in every story (c) never (d)
   more often than the perfect — a. FACT [REF:whitney1889 §826]
10. मा भैषीः is: (a) a chunk, an aorist taught whole (b) a future (c) an imperative (d) a perfect — a.

**§20 bookings (verbatim).**
- +1 day: the six ✓ rows — write the 3rd singular, dual and plural of भू in laṭ, laṅ, loṭ, vidhiliṅ, liṭ
  and lṛṭ from memory, naming each lakāra; v4.c04.l01's Station 0 runs exactly this.
- +1 week: class detective — for तिष्ठति, जायते, पृच्छति, शृणोति, अस्ति, ब्रवीति, युनक्ति, जानाति, चिन्तयति
  and स्वपिति name the class (by its first root), the class sign and the present stem, and give the rule
  of each one whose stem replaces the root, with the substitute-stem card open; v4.c04.l02's Station 0 runs exactly this.

**§21 Homework (40).** As the front matter: before — the assembly page from memory (8), the toolbox card
read once (3), §16 aloud (4); after — the quiz (7), the check (6), the spiral (4), remediation (5), the
real-world task (3): "explain to a Hindi speaker, in Hindi, why बभूव has neither vṛddhi nor guṇa and भवति has guṇa". = 40.

**Teacher Notes.** K43, K47 and the exam keys; the X1, X2, X8 quotations; the retake sets; the e-text's
*nahi* → न हि; the √भू table's Whitney sections (row 0.25) and the 1.x table's agreement in all 54 cells;
the D′ caveat (open item 5).

**Payoff (plan).** The √भू table: one root, all ten rows of the classical table, made by the tools of
three chapters. **Main risk (plan):** the ◐ rows treated as productive by stronger learners — the rubric
gives no credit for producing them; naming them is the target.

**Editor's decisions (2026-11-10, after the fact-base verification):**

The verification confirmed 30 findings (2 blockers, 9 major, 19 minor) and rejected 5; 65 fixes
were applied and 5 were duplicates between checkers. The two blockers, both fixed above: 6.4.111
श्नसोरल्लोपः drops the अ of श्न — the न that श्नम् puts inside a class-7 root — and of अस्, not the आ
of the class-9 sign श्ना (गृह्णन्ति is 6.4.112, गृह्णीतः 6.4.113); and पृच्छति was the wrong example of
a ṅit class sign blocking guṇa (its ऋ is neither final nor penultimate, so no guṇa could arise): the
example is लिखति, and पृच्छति keeps its own point, the samprasāraṇa of 6.1.16.

1. **The `dcs` key exists from this commit** (REFERENCES.md; search-confirmed, page not opened):
   items 6 and 9 are closed. Row 0.27's DCS lines print in learner text in their one wording, with
   [REF:dcs] and the method line; Teacher Notes name the scripts of `research/evidence/vol4-plan/`.

## 9. Errata to shipped files

### (a) Lines now false, with exact mechanical fixes

Searched 2026-11-10: every file of `curriculum/volume-1`, `volume-2` and `volume-3` for "v4.c03",
"Volume 4", "for v4", "ubhaya", "both families", "one family", "remote", "recent", "habitual",
"benefits oneself", "तिष्ठ-", "learned whole", "आह", "उवाच", "ददौ", "करिष्यामि", "गमिष्यति", "R4". One
line is made false by this chapter:

| File | Quote (verbatim, unique) | Replacement |
|---|---|---|
| `curriculum/volume-3/chapter-03/lesson-02-the-second-family.md` (l. 673–674) | `the course teaches every verb in one family only,` | `until v4.c03.l01 the course teaches every verb in one family only,` |

(After v4.c03.l01, पचति/पचते and यजति/यजते are taught in both sets; the line's next words, "the one
printed in its §14 row", stay true of every Volume 3 verb.)

### (c) What `curriculum/volume-4/volume.md` and the plan's §7.6 must say when the chapter ships

- **volume.md, the Chapter 3 row** is already right ("the l-affix and the pada from the root's marker;
  the ten present classes by their class sign; strong and weak by the p-marker; the perfect and the
  future; the √भू table of ten rows | 565"). When the chapter ships, the "planned figure" note for 565 is
  lifted, and a "Chapter 3 delivered" paragraph is added: six lessons — the root and the l-affix (1.3.1,
  3.2.123, 3.4.77–78, B), the pada from the root's marker (1.3.12, 1.3.72, Q; 1.3.78, 1.4.99, B),
  person and number (1.4.101–102, 1.4.108, 1.4.22, B), पठति and रोचते derived, IR-020 and the
  "benefits oneself" card corrected; the class sign (3.4.113, 3.1.68, 1.3.8, 7.3.84, B), the ten classes
  on one card, the substitute stems, नयति derived; strong and weak by the p-marker (1.2.4, 1.1.5, Q),
  शृणोति / शृण्वन्ति derived, the anubandha card, R4 closed, IR-022 corrected; the perfect (3.2.115, Q;
  T10), जगाम and ददौ derived, IR-019 corrected, the Hitopadeśa read (N1); the future (T12), करिष्यति
  derived, the card of the other four lakāras with the chunk मा भैषीः, the Pañcatantra read (N2); the
  Chapter 3 exam on the √भू table (one 45-minute sitting, Station 0 outside it; N8 unseen). The toolbox
  after Chapter 3: **89** tools. The ledger: **565** (28 ledger-new: 537 → 542 → 548 → 552 → 558 → 565 →
  565). The serial: episodes 1–5 (the wife's demand to the crocodile's resolve).
- **The plan's §7.6:** the nine rows of the table above, with "From: v4.c03 fact base".
